   249→---
   250→
   251→## Phase 1: Terraform Service Overlay
   252→
   253→This phase deploys infrastructure resources that overlay on top of the existing GCP infrastructure. Terraform creates service accounts, GCS buckets, secrets, Cloud Armor policies, SSL certificates, and database setup.
   254→
   255→### 1.1 Verify Existing Infrastructure
   256→
   257→```bash
   258→# Verify all three GKE clusters are accessible
   259→for env in dev qa prod; do
   260→  echo "=== Checking $env cluster ==="
   261→  gcloud container clusters describe kd-ix-eur-${env}-gke \
   262→    --region=europe-west1 \
   263→    --project=kd-ix-eur-${env}-gke \
   264→    --format="value(name,status)"
   265→done
   266→
   267→# Verify all three Cloud SQL instances exist
   268→for env in dev qa prod; do
   269→  echo "=== Checking Cloud SQL for $env ==="
   270→  gcloud sql instances describe kd-ix-eur-${env}-cloudsql-platform \
   271→    --project=kd-ix-eur-${env}-platform-data \
   272→    --format="value(name,state)"
   273→done
   274→
   275→# Verify VPC networks exist
   276→for env in dev qa prod; do
   277→  echo "=== Checking VPC for $env ==="
   278→  gcloud compute networks describe crewai-${env}-network \
   279→    --project=kd-ix-eur-${env}-network \
   280→    --format="value(name)"
   281→done
   282→```
   283→
   284→**Expected output**: All resources should show as RUNNABLE/READY.
   285→
   286→### 1.2 Configure Terraform Workspace
   287→
   288→```bash
   289→# Navigate to Terraform root
   290→cd terraform/root
   291→
   292→# Initialize Terraform (first time only)
   293→terraform init
   294→
   295→# Create workspaces for each environment
   296→terraform workspace new dev
   297→terraform workspace new qa
   298→terraform workspace new prod
   299→
   300→# Verify workspaces
   301→terraform workspace list
   302→# Output:
   303→#   default
   304→# * dev
   305→#   qa
   306→#   prod
   307→```
   308→
   309→### 1.3 Update Environment Configuration Files
   310→
   311→Each environment has a configuration file: `terraform/configs/{dev,qa,prod}.json`
   312→
   313→Review and update these files with your environment-specific values:
   314→
   315→```bash
   316→# View configuration structure
   317→cat terraform/configs/dev.json
   318→
   319→# Expected structure
   320→cat <<'EOF'
   321→{
   322→  "global": {
   323→    "project_id": "kd-ix-eur-dev-gke",
   324→    "platform_data_project": "kd-ix-eur-dev-platform-data",
   325→    "region": "europe-west1",
   326→    "environment": "dev"
   327→  },
   328→  "cloud_sql": {
   329→    "existing_instance_name": "kd-ix-eur-dev-cloudsql-platform"
   330→  },
   331→  "gcs_buckets": {
   332→    "data_bucket_name": "crewai-data-dev",
   333→    "logs_bucket_name": "crewai-logs-dev"
   334→  },
   335→  "ssl_certificates": {
   336→    "domains": ["crewai.dev.ix.konecta-digital.com"]
   337→  },
   338→  "cloud_armor": {
   339→    "rate_limit": 300
   340→  }
   341→}
   342→EOF
   343→
   344→# Update all three config files with your values
   345→# Be sure to verify:
   346→# - project_id matches your GCP projects
   347→# - database instance names match existing Cloud SQL instances
   348→# - GCS bucket names are globally unique
   349→# - SSL certificate domains match your DNS zone
   350→```
   351→
   352→Verify config files are valid JSON:
   353→```bash
   354→jq . terraform/configs/dev.json
   355→jq . terraform/configs/qa.json
   356→jq . terraform/configs/prod.json
   357→```
   358→
   359→### 1.4 Initialize Terraform Backend
   360→
   361→Terraform state must be stored in GCS for multi-environment management:
   362→
   363→```bash
   364→# Create state bucket (if not already created)
   365→gcloud storage buckets create gs://crewai-terraform-state \
   366→  --project=kd-ix-eur-dev-gke \
   367→  --location=europe-west1 \
   368→  --uniform-bucket-level-access \
   369→  --versioning
   370→
   371→# Enable versioning for backup
   372→gsutil versioning set on gs://crewai-terraform-state
   373→
   374→# Set up access logging (optional but recommended)
   375→gcloud storage buckets create gs://crewai-terraform-logs \
   376→  --project=kd-ix-eur-dev-gke \
   377→  --location=europe-west1
   378→
   379→gsutil logging set on -b gs://crewai-terraform-logs gs://crewai-terraform-state
   380→```
   381→
   382→Review `terraform/root/backend.tf` to ensure it points to your GCS bucket.
   383→
   384→### 1.5 Deploy Terraform - Development Environment
   385→
   386→```bash
   387→cd terraform/root
   388→
   389→# Select dev workspace
   390→terraform workspace select dev
   391→
   392→# Review Terraform plan
   393→terraform plan -var="environment=dev" > /tmp/tfplan-dev.txt
   394→
   395→# Review the plan before applying
   396→cat /tmp/tfplan-dev.txt | grep -E "(^[[:space:]]*\+|^[[:space:]]*-|^[[:space:]]*~)" | head -30
   397→
   398→# Apply Terraform
   399→terraform apply -var="environment=dev"
   400→
   401→# Expected output: Creates service accounts, GCS buckets, Secrets, Cloud Armor, SSL certificates, database setup
   402→```
   403→
   404→**What gets created:**
   405→- Service account: `crewai-platform-dev@kd-ix-eur-dev-gke.iam.gserviceaccount.com`
   406→- GCS buckets: `crewai-data-dev`, `crewai-logs-dev`
   407→- Secrets in Secret Manager: database credentials, GCS HMAC keys, OAuth
   408→- Cloud Armor policy: `dev-crewai-security-policy`
   409→- SSL certificate in Certificate Manager
   410→- Database users and schemas on existing Cloud SQL instance
   411→
   412→View outputs:
   413→```bash
   414→terraform output -json | jq .
   415→```
   416→
   417→### 1.6 Deploy Terraform - QA Environment
   418→
   419→```bash
   420→# Select qa workspace
   421→terraform workspace select qa
   422→
   423→# Review and apply
   424→terraform plan -var="environment=qa"
   425→terraform apply -var="environment=qa"
   426→```
   427→
   428→### 1.7 Deploy Terraform - Production Environment
   429→
   430→```bash
   431→# Select prod workspace
   432→terraform workspace select prod
   433→
   434→# Review and apply (may require approvals in CI/CD)
   435→terraform plan -var="environment=prod"
   436→terraform apply -var="environment=prod"
   437→```
   438→
   439→Reference: See `terraform/root/README.md` for detailed Terraform workflow documentation.
   440→
   441→---
   442→
   443→## Phase 2: Kubernetes Base Configuration
   444→
   445→This phase deploys the Kubernetes base configuration (namespaces, RBAC, network policies, resource quotas, Gateway API, External Secrets) on top of the existing GKE clusters using Kustomize.
   446→
   447→### 2.1 Get GKE Credentials
   448→
   449→```bash
   450→# Authenticate to each GKE cluster
   451→gcloud container clusters get-credentials kd-ix-eur-dev-gke \
   452→  --region=europe-west1 \
   453→  --project=kd-ix-eur-dev-gke
   454→
   455→gcloud container clusters get-credentials kd-ix-eur-qa-gke \
   456→  --region=europe-west1 \
   457→  --project=kd-ix-eur-qa-gke
   458→
   459→gcloud container clusters get-credentials kd-ix-eur-prod-gke \
   460→  --region=europe-west1 \
   461→  --project=kd-ix-eur-prod-gke
   462→
   463→# Verify connections
   464→kubectl cluster-info
   465→kubectl get nodes
   466→```
   467→
   468→### 2.2 Review Kustomize Structure
   469→
   470→```bash
   471→# Review the Kustomize directory structure
   472→tree kubernetes/kustomize/
   473→
   474→# Expected structure:
   475→# kubernetes/kustomize/
   476→# ├── base/                          # Common resources
   477→# │   ├── namespace.yaml             # Namespace with Pod Security labels
   478→# │   ├── role.yaml                  # RBAC role
   479→# │   ├── rolebinding.yaml           # RBAC role binding
   480→# │   ├── secret-store.yaml          # External Secrets SecretStore
   481→# │   ├── external-secret.yaml       # External Secrets ExternalSecret
   482→# │   ├── gateway.yaml               # Gateway API Gateway
   483→# │   ├── httproute.yaml             # Gateway API HTTPRoute
   484→# │   ├── backend-config.yaml        # Cloud Armor binding
   485→# │   ├── resource-quota.yaml        # Resource limits
   486→# │   ├── default-deny.yaml          # Network policy deny-all
   487→# │   ├── allow-web.yaml             # Network policy allow web
   488→# │   ├── allow-worker.yaml          # Network policy allow worker
   489→# │   ├── allow-buildkit.yaml        # Network policy allow buildkit
   490→# │   └── kustomization.yaml         # Base kustomization
   491→# └── overlays/                      # Environment-specific customizations
   492→#     ├── dev/kustomization.yaml
   493→#     ├── qa/kustomization.yaml
   494→#     └── prod/kustomization.yaml
   495→```
   496→
   497→Reference: See `kubernetes/kustomize/README.md` for detailed structure and customization patterns.
   498→
   499→### 2.3 Deploy Kubernetes Base Configuration - Development
   500→
   501→```bash
   502→# Switch to dev context
   503→kubectl config use-context gke_kd-ix-eur-dev-gke_europe-west1_kd-ix-eur-dev-gke
   504→
   505→# Build manifests (dry run)
   506→kubectl kustomize kubernetes/kustomize/overlays/dev | head -50
   507→
   508→# Validate against cluster (dry run)
   509→kubectl kustomize kubernetes/kustomize/overlays/dev | kubectl apply --dry-run=server -f -
   510→
   511→# Apply the manifests
   512→kubectl apply -k kubernetes/kustomize/overlays/dev
   513→
   514→# Verify deployment
   515→kubectl get namespace crewai-dev
   516→kubectl get role -n crewai-dev
   517→kubectl get rolebinding -n crewai-dev
   518→kubectl get networkpolicies -n crewai-dev
   519→kubectl get resourcequota -n crewai-dev
   520→kubectl get secretstore -n crewai-dev
   521→kubectl get externalsecret -n crewai-dev
   522→kubectl get gateway -n crewai-dev
   523→kubectl get httproute -n crewai-dev
   524→
   525→# Expected output:
   526→# - Namespace: crewai-dev with Pod Security labels
   527→# - RBAC: crewai-platform-role and crewai-platform-rolebinding
   528→# - Network Policies: default-deny, allow-web, allow-worker, allow-buildkit
   529→# - Resource Quota: crewai-resource-quota
   530→# - External Secrets: gcp-secret-manager (SecretStore) and crewai-secrets (ExternalSecret)
   531→# - Gateway API: crewai-dev-gateway and crewai-dev-httproute
   532→```
   533→
   534→### 2.4 Deploy Kubernetes Base Configuration - QA
   535→
   536→```bash
   537→# Switch to qa context
   538→kubectl config use-context gke_kd-ix-eur-qa-gke_europe-west1_kd-ix-eur-qa-gke
   539→
   540→# Apply the manifests
   541→kubectl apply -k kubernetes/kustomize/overlays/qa
   542→
   543→# Verify
   544→kubectl get namespace crewai-qa
   545→```
   546→
   547→### 2.5 Deploy Kubernetes Base Configuration - Production
   548→
   549→```bash
   550→# Switch to prod context
   551→kubectl config use-context gke_kd-ix-eur-prod-gke_europe-west1_kd-ix-eur-prod-gke
   552→
   553→# Apply the manifests
   554→kubectl apply -k kubernetes/kustomize/overlays/prod
   555→
   556→# Verify
   557→kubectl get namespace crewai-prod
   558→
   559→# Production uses the most restrictive Pod Security policies
   560→kubectl get namespace crewai-prod -o yaml | grep pod-security
   561→```
   562→
   563→### 2.6 Verify External Secrets Configuration
   564→
   565→External Secrets Operator must be installed on each cluster before ExternalSecret resources can sync:
   566→
   567→```bash
   568→# Check if External Secrets Operator is installed
   569→for env in dev qa prod; do
   570→  echo "=== Checking External Secrets for $env ==="
   571→  case $env in
   572→    dev) context="gke_kd-ix-eur-dev-gke_europe-west1_kd-ix-eur-dev-gke" ;;
   573→    qa) context="gke_kd-ix-eur-qa-gke_europe-west1_kd-ix-eur-qa-gke" ;;
   574→    prod) context="gke_kd-ix-eur-prod-gke_europe-west1_kd-ix-eur-prod-gke" ;;
   575→  esac
   576→
   577→  kubectl config use-context "$context"
   578→  kubectl get deployment -n external-secrets-system
   579→done
   580→
   581→# If not installed, add the External Secrets Helm repository and install
   582→helm repo add external-secrets https://charts.external-secrets.io
   583→helm repo update
   584→
   585→# Install for each environment
   586→for env in dev qa prod; do
   587→  echo "=== Installing External Secrets for $env ==="
   588→  case $env in
   589→    dev) context="gke_kd-ix-eur-dev-gke_europe-west1_kd-ix-eur-dev-gke" ;;
   590→    qa) context="gke_kd-ix-eur-qa-gke_europe-west1_kd-ix-eur-qa-gke" ;;
   591→    prod) context="gke_kd-ix-eur-prod-gke_europe-west1_kd-ix-eur-prod-gke" ;;
   592→  esac
   593→
   594→  kubectl config use-context "$context"
   595→
   596→  helm install external-secrets \
   597→    external-secrets/external-secrets \
   598→    --namespace external-secrets-system \
   599→    --create-namespace \
   600→    --set installCRDs=true
   601→done
   602→```
   603→
   604→### 2.7 Create Workload Identity Bindings
   605→
   606→Kubernetes service accounts must be bound to GCP service accounts via Workload Identity:
   607→
   608→```bash
   609→# For each environment, bind the Kubernetes SA to the GCP SA
   610→for env in dev qa prod; do
   611→  echo "=== Setting up Workload Identity for $env ==="
   612→  case $env in
   613→    dev) context="gke_kd-ix-eur-dev-gke_europe-west1_kd-ix-eur-dev-gke"; project="kd-ix-eur-dev-gke" ;;
   614→    qa) context="gke_kd-ix-eur-qa-gke_europe-west1_kd-ix-eur-qa-gke"; project="kd-ix-eur-qa-gke" ;;
   615→    prod) context="gke_kd-ix-eur-prod-gke_europe-west1_kd-ix-eur-prod-gke"; project="kd-ix-eur-prod-gke" ;;
   616→  esac
   617→
   618→  kubectl config use-context "$context"
   619→
   620→  # Add Workload Identity annotation to service account
   621→  kubectl annotate serviceaccount crewai-platform-sa \
   622→    -n crewai-${env} \
   623→    iam.gke.io/gcp-service-account=crewai-platform-${env}@${project}.iam.gserviceaccount.com \
   624→    --overwrite
   625→
   626→  # Bind GCP service account to Kubernetes service account
   627→  gcloud iam service-accounts add-iam-policy-binding \
   628→    crewai-platform-${env}@${project}.iam.gserviceaccount.com \
   629→    --role=roles/iam.workloadIdentityUser \
   630→    --member="serviceAccount:${project}.svc.id.goog[crewai-${env}/crewai-platform-sa]" \
   631→    --project=${project}
   632→done
   633→```
   634→
   635→---
   636→
   637→## Phase 3: ArgoCD Application Deployment
   638→
   639→This phase deploys the CrewAI application and Velero backup configuration via ArgoCD using the **App of Apps GitOps pattern**. ArgoCD is already installed on each cluster.
   640→
   641→### Quick Summary: Three Approaches
   642→
   643→| Approach | Method | Best For | Time | kubectl? |
   644→|----------|--------|----------|------|----------|
   645→| **Recommended** | ArgoCD Web UI + Root App | Visual setup, first-time users | 10 min | No |
   646→| **Recommended** | ArgoCD CLI + Root App | Scripting, automation, CI/CD | 5 min | No |
   647→| **Alternative** | REST API + Root App | Custom integrations, webhooks | 10 min | No |
   648→| **Legacy** | kubectl apply (manual) | Advanced K8s admins only | 5 min | Yes |
   649→
   650→### The GitOps Way: App of Apps Pattern
   651→
   652→Instead of managing individual applications, we use the **App of Apps pattern**:
   653→
   654→1. **One bootstrap application** (`crewai-root`) that monitors Git
   655→2. **Automatic discovery** of all apps in `argocd/applications/`
   656→3. **GitOps forever** - manage everything via Git commits
   657→4. **No kubectl needed** - use ArgoCD UI/CLI exclusively
   658→
   659→**Benefits:**
   660→- ✅ True GitOps: Single source of truth in Git
   661→- ✅ Scalable: Add new environments by committing YAML to Git
   662→- ✅ Auditable: Every change tracked in Git history
   663→- ✅ Declartive: State matches Git exactly
   664→- ✅ No kubectl: All operations via ArgoCD UI/CLI/API
   665→
   666→### Choose Your Bootstrap Method
   667→
   668→**For comprehensive instructions**, see [`argocd/bootstrap/UI-API-SETUP.md`](../argocd/bootstrap/UI-API-SETUP.md) which includes:
   669→- Complete UI walkthrough with screenshots
   670→- Scriptable CLI automation
   671→- REST API examples with curl
   672→- Comparison of UI vs CLI vs API
   673→- Troubleshooting guides
   674→
   675→---
   676→
   677→### 3.0 Bootstrap via ArgoCD (UI/CLI) - Recommended
   678→
   679→**Choose your preferred method:**
   680→
   681→#### Option A: ArgoCD Web UI (Most User-Friendly)
   682→
   683→Perfect for visual learners and first-time setup.
   684→
   685→```bash
   686→# Access ArgoCD UI
   687→# If not exposed externally, port-forward:
   688→kubectl port-forward svc/argocd-server -n argocd 8080:443
   689→
   690→# Or access directly:
   691→# https://argocd.your-domain.com
   692→```
   693→
   694→**Steps in UI:**
   695→
   696→1. **Login**: `admin` / Get password via `argocd admin initial-password -n argocd`
   697→
   698→2. **Add Repository** (Settings → Repositories → Connect Repo):
   699→   - Repository URL: `https://github.com/konecta-ix-services/kix-services-crewai.git`
   700→   - Click "Connect"
   701→
   702→3. **Create Root Application** (Applications → + New App):
   703→   - **Application Name**: `crewai-root`
   704→   - **Project**: `default`
   705→   - **Sync Policy**: `Automatic` (check Prune & Self Heal)
   706→   - **Repository URL**: `https://github.com/konecta-ix-services/kix-services-crewai.git`
   707→   - **Revision**: `main`
   708→   - **Path**: `argocd/applications`
   709→   - **Cluster URL**: `https://kubernetes.default.svc`
   710→   - **Namespace**: `argocd`
   711→   - Click **Create**
   712→
   713→4. **Wait for Discovery** (30-60 seconds):
   714→   - Root app discovers child apps: `crewai-platform-dev`, `crewai-platform-qa`, `crewai-platform-prod`
   715→
   716→5. **Sync Each Environment**:
   717→   - Click each app → Click **Sync** button → Click **Synchronize**
   718→   - Watch real-time resource creation in the visual tree
   719→
   720→✅ **Done! All future changes happen via Git commits.**
   721→
   722→---
   723→
   724→#### Option B: ArgoCD CLI (Most Scriptable)
   725→
   726→Perfect for automation and CI/CD pipelines.
   727→
   728→```bash
   729→# Install ArgoCD CLI (if not already installed)
   730→# Linux: curl -sSL -o argocd https://github.com/argoproj/argo-cd/releases/latest/download/argocd-linux-amd64
   731→# macOS: brew install argocd
   732→# Windows: scoop install argocd
   733→
   734→# Login to ArgoCD
   735→argocd login argocd.your-domain.com --username admin --password <password>
   736→
   737→# Or if using port-forward:
   738→kubectl port-forward svc/argocd-server -n argocd 8080:443 &
   739→argocd login localhost:8080 --username admin --password <password> --insecure
   740→
   741→# Add Git repository
   742→argocd repo add https://github.com/konecta-ix-services/kix-services-crewai.git
   743→
   744→# Create root application (App of Apps)
   745→argocd app create crewai-root \
   746→  --repo https://github.com/konecta-ix-services/kix-services-crewai.git \
   747→  --path argocd/applications \
   748→  --dest-server https://kubernetes.default.svc \
   749→  --dest-namespace argocd \
   750→  --sync-policy automated \
   751→  --auto-prune \
   752→  --self-heal \
   753→  --project default
   754→
   755→# Sync root application
   756→argocd app sync crewai-root
   757→argocd app wait crewai-root --health
   758→
   759→# Wait for child apps to be discovered (60 seconds)
   760→sleep 60
   761→
   762→# Sync all child applications
   763→argocd app sync crewai-platform-dev --async
   764→argocd app sync crewai-platform-qa --async
   765→argocd app sync crewai-platform-prod --async
   766→
   767→# Wait for health
   768→argocd app wait crewai-platform-dev --health --timeout 600
   769→argocd app wait crewai-platform-qa --health --timeout 600
   770→argocd app wait crewai-platform-prod --health --timeout 600
   771→
   772→# Verify all apps
   773→argocd app list | grep crewai
   774→```
   775→
   776→✅ **Done! Fully scriptable and no kubectl needed.**
   777→
   778→---
   779→
   780→#### Complete Automation Script
   781→
   782→For a complete, ready-to-run script, see [`argocd/bootstrap/UI-API-SETUP.md`](../argocd/bootstrap/UI-API-SETUP.md) which includes:
   783→- Full CLI automation script
   784→- REST API examples with curl
   785→- Troubleshooting guides
   786→- Comparison of UI vs CLI vs API approaches
   787→
   788→---
   789→
   790→### 3.0 (Alternative) Bootstrap via kubectl
   791→
   792→**⚠️ Skip this section if you used ArgoCD UI/CLI above.**
   793→
   794→For users who prefer direct cluster access via kubectl:
   795→
   796→#### Bootstrap Setup
   797→
   798→```bash
   799→# Update Git repository URL in the bootstrap configuration
   800→sed -i 's|https://github.com/konecta-ix-services/kix-services-crewai.git|https://github.com/YOUR-ORG/YOUR-REPO.git|g' \
   801→  argocd/bootstrap/root-app.yaml
   802→
   803→# Apply the root application to any one cluster (it manages itself and all child apps)
   804→kubectl config use-context gke_kd-ix-eur-dev-gke_europe-west1_kd-ix-eur-dev-gke
   805→kubectl apply -f argocd/bootstrap/root-app.yaml
   806→
   807→# Verify root application is synced
   808→argocd app get crewai-root
   809→
   810→# Wait for root app to create child applications (30-60 seconds)
   811→watch -n 2 'argocd app list | grep crewai'
   812→
   813→# Expected output:
   814→# crewai-root              argocd  https://github.com/...  main  argocd            Synced  Healthy
   815→# crewai-platform-dev      argocd  oci://registry.crewai.. 0.3.10  crewai-dev      OutOfSync  Missing
   816→# crewai-platform-qa       argocd  oci://registry.crewai.. 0.3.10  crewai-qa       OutOfSync  Missing
   817→# crewai-platform-prod     argocd  oci://registry.crewai.. 0.3.10  crewai-prod     OutOfSync  Missing
   818→```
   819→
   820→#### Sync Child Applications
   821→
   822→ArgoCD has now created all child applications. Sync them in order:
   823→
   824→```bash
   825→# Sync development environment first
   826→argocd app sync crewai-platform-dev --async
   827→
   828→# Wait for dev to be healthy (5-10 minutes for all pods)
   829→argocd app wait crewai-platform-dev --health
   830→
   831→# Sync QA
   832→argocd app sync crewai-platform-qa --async
   833→argocd app wait crewai-platform-qa --health
   834→
   835→# Sync production (manually or via CI/CD)
   836→argocd app sync crewai-platform-prod --async
   837→argocd app wait crewai-platform-prod --health
   838→```
   839→
   840→#### Verify Bootstrap Success
   841→
   842→```bash
   843→# Check all applications are managed by root app
   844→argocd app list --selector argocd.argoproj.io/instance=crewai-root
   845→
   846→# Check sync status
   847→argocd app get crewai-platform-dev
   848→argocd app get crewai-platform-qa
   849→argocd app get crewai-platform-prod
   850→
   851→# Expected status for each: Synced, Healthy
   852→```
   853→
   854→> **Future Application Management**: After bootstrap, all application management happens via Git commits. Add new environments by creating YAML files in `argocd/applications/` and committing to Git. See [`argocd/bootstrap/README.md`](../argocd/bootstrap/README.md) for examples.
   855→
   856→---
   857→
   858→### 3.1 Manual Deployment (Alternative to Bootstrap)
   859→
   860→**⚠️ Skip this section if you used the bootstrap approach above.**
   861→
   862→If you prefer manual control or are troubleshooting, you can apply applications individually:
   863→
   864→#### 3.1.1 Review ArgoCD Applications
   865→
   866→```bash
   867→# Review existing ArgoCD applications
   868→ls -la argocd/applications/
   869→
   870→# Review the Dev application structure
   871→cat argocd/applications/crewai-dev.yaml
   872→
   873→# Expected structure:
   874→# - Source 1: Git repository path (Helm chart)
   875→# - Source 2: Git repository paths (Kubernetes manifests: Gateway API, External Secrets, Velero)
   876→# - Sync waves for proper resource ordering
   877→# - Sync policy (auto or manual)
   878→```
   879→
   880→Reference: See the actual application files for detailed configuration:
   881→- `argocd/applications/crewai-dev.yaml`
   882→- `argocd/applications/crewai-qa.yaml`
   883→- `argocd/applications/crewai-prod.yaml`
   884→
   885→#### 3.1.2 Update Git Repository URL
   886→
   887→Before deploying, update the Git repository URL in the ArgoCD applications:
   888→
   889→```bash
   890→# Edit the applications to point to your Git repository
   891→sed -i 's|https://github.com/your-org/kix-services-crewai.git|https://github.com/your-org/kix-services-crewai.git|g' \
   892→  argocd/applications/crewai-*.yaml
   893→
   894→# Verify the change
   895→grep "repoURL:" argocd/applications/crewai-dev.yaml
   896→```
   897→
   898→#### 3.1.3 Deploy ArgoCD Application - Development
   899→
   900→```bash
   901→# Switch to dev context
   902→kubectl config use-context gke_kd-ix-eur-dev-gke_europe-west1_kd-ix-eur-dev-gke
   903→
   904→# Verify ArgoCD is running
   905→kubectl get pods -n argocd
   906→
   907→# Apply the ArgoCD application
   908→kubectl apply -f argocd/applications/crewai-dev.yaml
   909→
   910→# Monitor deployment progress
   911→kubectl get application -n argocd
   912→argocd app get crewai-platform-dev
   913→
   914→# Wait for application to sync (may take 2-5 minutes)
   915→# Wave 0: External Secrets SecretStore
   916→# Wave 1: External Secrets ExternalSecret
   917→# Wave 2: Gateway API BackendConfig
   918→# Wave 3: Gateway API Gateway
   919→# Wave 4: Gateway API HTTPRoute
   920→# Wave 5: CrewAI Platform Helm chart (web, worker, buildkit pods)
   921→
   922→# Watch pods coming up
   923→kubectl get pods -n crewai-dev -w
   924→```
   925→
   926→#### 3.1.4 Deploy ArgoCD Application - QA
   927→
   928→```bash
   929→# Switch to qa context
   930→kubectl config use-context gke_kd-ix-eur-qa-gke_europe-west1_kd-ix-eur-qa-gke
   931→
   932→# Apply the application
   933→kubectl apply -f argocd/applications/crewai-qa.yaml
   934→
   935→# Monitor
   936→kubectl get application -n argocd
   937→kubectl get pods -n crewai-qa -w
   938→```
   939→
   940→#### 3.1.5 Deploy ArgoCD Application - Production
   941→
   942→```bash
   943→# Switch to prod context
   944→kubectl config use-context gke_kd-ix-eur-prod-gke_europe-west1_kd-ix-eur-prod-gke
   945→
   946→# Apply the application
   947→kubectl apply -f argocd/applications/crewai-prod.yaml
   948→
   949→# Monitor
   950→kubectl get application -n argocd
   951→kubectl get pods -n crewai-prod -w
   952→```
   953→
   954→### 3.2 Verify Secrets Synchronization
   955→
   956→```bash
   957→# For each environment, verify secrets are synced from GCP Secret Manager
   958→for env in dev qa prod; do
   959→  echo "=== Checking secrets for $env ==="
   960→  case $env in
   961→    dev) context="gke_kd-ix-eur-dev-gke_europe-west1_kd-ix-eur-dev-gke" ;;
   962→    qa) context="gke_kd-ix-eur-qa-gke_europe-west1_kd-ix-eur-qa-gke" ;;
   963→    prod) context="gke_kd-ix-eur-prod-gke_europe-west1_kd-ix-eur-prod-gke" ;;
   964→  esac
   965→
   966→  kubectl config use-context "$context"
   967→
   968→  # Check ExternalSecret status
   969→  kubectl describe externalsecret crewai-secrets -n crewai-${env}
   970→
   971→  # Check if secret was created in Kubernetes
   972→  kubectl get secret crewai-secrets -n crewai-${env}
   973→
   974→  # Expected: Status should show "SecretSynced" and secret should exist
   975→done
   976→```
   977→
   978→---
   979→
   980→## Phase 4: CI/CD Pipeline Setup
   981→
   982→This phase configures GitHub Actions for automated Terraform and Kubernetes deployments.
   983→
   984→### 4.1 Configure GitHub Environments
   985→
   986→1. Go to your GitHub repository settings
   987→2. Navigate to **Settings** > **Environments**
   988→3. Create three environments:
   989→
   990→#### Development Environment
   991→- **Environment name:** `dev`
   992→- **Deployment branches:** `main` only
   993→- **Required reviewers:** None
   994→
   995→#### QA Environment
   996→- **Environment name:** `qa`
   997→- **Deployment branches:** `main` only
   998→- **Required reviewers:** 1 (optional)
   999→
  1000→#### Production Environment
  1001→- **Environment name:** `production`
  1002→- **Deployment branches:** `main` only
  1003→- **Required reviewers:** 2 from `platform-team` (required)
  1004→
  1005→### 4.2 Set Up GCP Workload Identity for GitHub Actions
  1006→
  1007→For each environment, create a GitHub Actions service account with Workload Identity:
  1008→
  1009→```bash
  1010→# Development
  1011→export PROJECT_ID="kd-ix-eur-dev-gke"
  1012→export GITHUB_REPO="grupokonecta/kix-services-crewai"  # Update with your org/repo
  1013→export SA_NAME="github-actions-sa"
  1014→
  1015→gcloud config set project $PROJECT_ID
  1016→export PROJECT_NUMBER=$(gcloud projects describe $PROJECT_ID --format="value(projectNumber)")
  1017→
  1018→echo "Project Number: $PROJECT_NUMBER"
  1019→
  1020→# Create service account
  1021→gcloud iam service-accounts create $SA_NAME \
  1022→  --display-name="GitHub Actions CI/CD Service Account"
  1023→
  1024→# Grant necessary roles
  1025→for role in container.admin compute.admin cloudsql.client secretmanager.admin storage.admin; do
  1026→  gcloud projects add-iam-policy-binding $PROJECT_ID \
  1027→    --member="serviceAccount:${SA_NAME}@${PROJECT_ID}.iam.gserviceaccount.com" \
  1028→    --role="roles/${role}"
  1029→done
  1030→
  1031→# Enable required APIs
  1032→gcloud services enable iamcredentials.googleapis.com sts.googleapis.com cloudresourcemanager.googleapis.com
  1033→
  1034→# Create Workload Identity Pool
  1035→gcloud iam workload-identity-pools create "github-pool" \
  1036→  --location="global" \
  1037→  --display-name="GitHub Actions Pool"
  1038→
  1039→# Create OIDC Provider
  1040→gcloud iam workload-identity-pools providers create-oidc "github-provider" \
  1041→  --location="global" \
  1042→  --workload-identity-pool="github-pool" \
  1043→  --display-name="GitHub OIDC Provider" \
  1044→  --attribute-mapping="google.subject=assertion.sub,attribute.actor=assertion.actor,attribute.repository=assertion.repository" \
  1045→  --issuer-uri="https://token.actions.githubusercontent.com"
  1046→
  1047→# Allow GitHub to impersonate service account
  1048→gcloud iam service-accounts add-iam-policy-binding \
  1049→  "${SA_NAME}@${PROJECT_ID}.iam.gserviceaccount.com" \
  1050→  --role="roles/iam.workloadIdentityUser" \
  1051→  --member="principalSet://iam.googleapis.com/projects/${PROJECT_NUMBER}/locations/global/workloadIdentityPools/github-pool/attribute.repository/${GITHUB_REPO}"
  1052→
  1053→# Get Workload Identity Provider resource name
  1054→export WI_PROVIDER=$(gcloud iam workload-identity-pools providers describe "github-provider" \
  1055→  --location="global" \
  1056→  --workload-identity-pool="github-pool" \
  1057→  --format="value(name)")
  1058→
  1059→echo "Save for GitHub Secrets:"
  1060→echo "GCP_WORKLOAD_IDENTITY_PROVIDER_DEV=$WI_PROVIDER"
  1061→echo "GCP_SERVICE_ACCOUNT_DEV=${SA_NAME}@${PROJECT_ID}.iam.gserviceaccount.com"
  1062→
  1063→# Repeat for QA and Production with PROJECT_ID="kd-ix-eur-qa-gke" and PROJECT_ID="kd-ix-eur-prod-gke"
  1064→```
  1065→
  1066→### 4.3 Configure GitHub Secrets
  1067→
  1068→Add the following secrets to your repository:
  1069→
  1070→```bash
  1071→# Using GitHub CLI:
  1072→gh secret set GCP_WORKLOAD_IDENTITY_PROVIDER_DEV -b "projects/XXX/locations/global/..."
  1073→gh secret set GCP_SERVICE_ACCOUNT_DEV -b "github-actions-sa@kd-ix-eur-dev-gke.iam.gserviceaccount.com"
  1074→
  1075→gh secret set GCP_WORKLOAD_IDENTITY_PROVIDER_QA -b "projects/XXX/locations/global/..."
  1076→gh secret set GCP_SERVICE_ACCOUNT_QA -b "github-actions-sa@kd-ix-eur-qa-gke.iam.gserviceaccount.com"
  1077→
  1078→gh secret set GCP_WORKLOAD_IDENTITY_PROVIDER_PROD -b "projects/XXX/locations/global/..."
  1079→gh secret set GCP_SERVICE_ACCOUNT_PROD -b "github-actions-sa@kd-ix-eur-prod-gke.iam.gserviceaccount.com"
  1080→```
  1081→
  1082→| Secret Name | Value | Description |
  1083→|------------|-------|-------------|
  1084→| `GCP_WORKLOAD_IDENTITY_PROVIDER_DEV` | From step 4.2 | Dev Workload Identity Provider |
  1085→| `GCP_SERVICE_ACCOUNT_DEV` | From step 4.2 | Dev service account email |
  1086→| `GCP_WORKLOAD_IDENTITY_PROVIDER_QA` | From step 4.2 | QA Workload Identity Provider |
  1087→| `GCP_SERVICE_ACCOUNT_QA` | From step 4.2 | QA service account email |
  1088→| `GCP_WORKLOAD_IDENTITY_PROVIDER_PROD` | From step 4.2 | Prod Workload Identity Provider |
  1089→| `GCP_SERVICE_ACCOUNT_PROD` | From step 4.2 | Prod service account email |
  1090→
  1091→### 4.4 Configure Branch Protection
  1092→
  1093→1. Go to **Settings** > **Branches**
  1094→2. Add branch protection rule for `main`:
  1095→   - Require pull request before merging
  1096→   - Require at least 1 approval
  1097→   - Require status checks to pass
  1098→   - Require branches to be up to date
  1099→
  1100→### 4.5 Create Platform Team
  1101→
  1102→1. Go to your GitHub organization
  1103→2. Navigate to **Teams**
  1104→3. Create a new team: `platform-team`
  1105→4. Add team members (minimum 2 people for prod approval)
  1106→5. Grant the team **Write** access to the repository
  1107→
  1108→---
  1109→
  1110→## Phase 5: Verification & Testing
  1111→
  1112→This phase verifies that all components are properly deployed and functioning.
  1113→
  1114→### 5.1 Verify Terraform Resources
  1115→
  1116→```bash
  1117→# For each environment, verify Terraform created all resources
  1118→for env in dev qa prod; do
  1119→  echo "=== Verifying Terraform for $env ==="
  1120→
  1121→  project_id="kd-ix-eur-${env}-gke"
  1122→
  1123→  # Verify service account
  1124→  gcloud iam service-accounts describe \
  1125→    crewai-platform-${env}@${project_id}.iam.gserviceaccount.com \
  1126→    --project=${project_id} 2>/dev/null || echo "Service account not found"
  1127→
  1128→  # Verify GCS buckets
  1129→  gcloud storage buckets describe gs://crewai-data-${env} 2>/dev/null || echo "Data bucket not found"
  1130→  gcloud storage buckets describe gs://crewai-logs-${env} 2>/dev/null || echo "Logs bucket not found"
  1131→
  1132→  # Verify secrets
  1133→  gcloud secrets list --filter="name:crewai-${env}" --project=${project_id} 2>/dev/null || echo "Secrets not found"
  1134→
  1135→  # Verify Cloud Armor policy
  1136→  gcloud compute security-policies describe ${env}-crewai-security-policy --project=${project_id} 2>/dev/null || echo "Cloud Armor policy not found"
  1137→
  1138→  echo "---"
  1139→done
  1140→```
  1141→
  1142→### 5.2 Verify Kubernetes Resources
  1143→
  1144→```bash
  1145→# For each environment, verify Kubernetes resources are deployed
  1146→for env in dev qa prod; do
  1147→  echo "=== Verifying Kubernetes for $env ==="
  1148→  case $env in
  1149→    dev) context="gke_kd-ix-eur-dev-gke_europe-west1_kd-ix-eur-dev-gke" ;;
  1150→    qa) context="gke_kd-ix-eur-qa-gke_europe-west1_kd-ix-eur-qa-gke" ;;
  1151→    prod) context="gke_kd-ix-eur-prod-gke_europe-west1_kd-ix-eur-prod-gke" ;;
  1152→  esac
  1153→
  1154→  kubectl config use-context "$context"
  1155→
  1156→  # Check namespace
  1157→  kubectl get namespace crewai-${env}
  1158→
  1159→  # Check RBAC
  1160→  kubectl get role,rolebinding -n crewai-${env}
  1161→
  1162→  # Check Network Policies
  1163→  kubectl get networkpolicies -n crewai-${env}
  1164→
  1165→  # Check Resource Quota
  1166→  kubectl get resourcequota -n crewai-${env}
  1167→
  1168→  # Check External Secrets
  1169→  kubectl get secretstore,externalsecret -n crewai-${env}
  1170→
  1171→  # Check Gateway API
  1172→  kubectl get gateway,httproute -n crewai-${env}
  1173→
  1174→  echo "---"
  1175→done
  1176→```
  1177→
  1178→### 5.3 Verify ArgoCD Applications
  1179→
  1180→```bash
  1181→# For each environment, verify ArgoCD application is synced
  1182→for env in dev qa prod; do
  1183→  echo "=== Verifying ArgoCD for $env ==="
  1184→  case $env in
  1185→    dev) context="gke_kd-ix-eur-dev-gke_europe-west1_kd-ix-eur-dev-gke" ;;
  1186→    qa) context="gke_kd-ix-eur-qa-gke_europe-west1_kd-ix-eur-qa-gke" ;;
  1187→    prod) context="gke_kd-ix-eur-prod-gke_europe-west1_kd-ix-eur-prod-gke" ;;
  1188→  esac
  1189→
  1190→  kubectl config use-context "$context"
  1191→
  1192→  # Check application status
  1193→  kubectl get application crewai-platform-${env} -n argocd -o jsonpath='{.status.sync.status}{"\n"}'
  1194→
  1195→  # Get application health
  1196→  kubectl get application crewai-platform-${env} -n argocd -o jsonpath='{.status.health.status}{"\n"}'
  1197→
  1198→  # Expected: Synced and Healthy
  1199→done
  1200→```
  1201→
  1202→### 5.4 Verify External Secrets Synchronization
  1203→
  1204→```bash
  1205→# Verify secrets are synced from GCP Secret Manager to Kubernetes
  1206→for env in dev qa prod; do
  1207→  echo "=== Verifying External Secrets for $env ==="
  1208→  case $env in
  1209→    dev) context="gke_kd-ix-eur-dev-gke_europe-west1_kd-ix-eur-dev-gke" ;;
  1210→    qa) context="gke_kd-ix-eur-qa-gke_europe-west1_kd-ix-eur-qa-gke" ;;
  1211→    prod) context="gke_kd-ix-eur-prod-gke_europe-west1_kd-ix-eur-prod-gke" ;;
  1212→  esac
  1213→
  1214→  kubectl config use-context "$context"
  1215→
  1216→  # Check ExternalSecret status
  1217→  kubectl get externalsecret crewai-secrets -n crewai-${env} -o jsonpath='{.status.conditions[0].type}{": "}{.status.conditions[0].status}{"\n"}'
  1218→
  1219→  # Check synced secret
  1220→  kubectl get secret crewai-secrets -n crewai-${env} -o jsonpath='{.type}{"\n"}'
  1221→
  1222→  # Expected: SecretSynced and Opaque
  1223→done
  1224→```
  1225→
  1226→### 5.5 Verify Gateway API and SSL Certificates
  1227→
  1228→```bash
  1229→# Verify Gateway API resources and SSL certificates
  1230→for env in dev qa prod; do
  1231→  echo "=== Verifying Gateway API for $env ==="
  1232→  case $env in
  1233→    dev) context="gke_kd-ix-eur-dev-gke_europe-west1_kd-ix-eur-dev-gke"; domain="crewai.dev.ix.konecta-digital.com" ;;
  1234→    qa) context="gke_kd-ix-eur-qa-gke_europe-west1_kd-ix-eur-qa-gke"; domain="crewai.qa.ix.konecta-digital.com" ;;
  1235→    prod) context="gke_kd-ix-eur-prod-gke_europe-west1_kd-ix-eur-prod-gke"; domain="crewai.ix.konecta-digital.com" ;;
  1236→  esac
  1237→
  1238→  kubectl config use-context "$context"
  1239→
  1240→  # Check Gateway
  1241→  echo "Gateway status:"
  1242→  kubectl get gateway crewai-${env}-gateway -n crewai-${env} -o jsonpath='{.status.conditions[0].status}{"\n"}'
  1243→
  1244→  # Get external IP
  1245→  external_ip=$(kubectl get gateway crewai-${env}-gateway -n crewai-${env} -o jsonpath='{.status.addresses[0].value}')
  1246→  echo "External IP: $external_ip"
  1247→
  1248→  # Check HTTPRoute
  1249→  echo "HTTPRoute status:"
  1250→  kubectl get httproute crewai-${env}-httproute -n crewai-${env} -o jsonpath='{.status.parents[0].conditions[0].status}{"\n"}'
  1251→
  1252→  # Verify SSL certificate (once DNS is configured)
  1253→  # openssl s_client -connect ${domain}:443 -servername ${domain} </dev/null 2>/dev/null | grep "Issuer:"
  1254→
  1255→  echo "---"
  1256→done
  1257→```
  1258→
  1259→### 5.6 Verify Application Pods
  1260→
  1261→```bash
  1262→# Verify CrewAI application pods are running
  1263→for env in dev qa prod; do
  1264→  echo "=== Verifying Application Pods for $env ==="
  1265→  case $env in
  1266→    dev) context="gke_kd-ix-eur-dev-gke_europe-west1_kd-ix-eur-dev-gke" ;;
  1267→    qa) context="gke_kd-ix-eur-qa-gke_europe-west1_kd-ix-eur-qa-gke" ;;
  1268→    prod) context="gke_kd-ix-eur-prod-gke_europe-west1_kd-ix-eur-prod-gke" ;;
  1269→  esac
  1270→
  1271→  kubectl config use-context "$context"
  1272→
  1273→  # List all pods in crewai namespace
  1274→  kubectl get pods -n crewai-${env}
  1275→
  1276→  # Check pod status
  1277→  kubectl get pods -n crewai-${env} -o jsonpath='{.items[*].status.containerStatuses[*].ready}{"\n"}'
  1278→
  1279→  # Expected: All pods should be Running and Ready (1/1)
  1280→done
  1281→```
  1282→
  1283→### 5.7 Verify Cloud SQL Connectivity
  1284→
  1285→```bash
  1286→# Verify pods can connect to Cloud SQL
  1287→for env in dev qa prod; do
  1288→  echo "=== Testing Cloud SQL connectivity for $env ==="
  1289→  case $env in
  1290→    dev) context="gke_kd-ix-eur-dev-gke_europe-west1_kd-ix-eur-dev-gke"; project="kd-ix-eur-dev-platform-data" ;;
  1291→    qa) context="gke_kd-ix-eur-qa-gke_europe-west1_kd-ix-eur-qa-gke"; project="kd-ix-eur-qa-platform-data" ;;
  1292→    prod) context="gke_kd-ix-eur-prod-gke_europe-west1_kd-ix-eur-prod-gke"; project="kd-ix-eur-prod-platform-data" ;;
  1293→  esac
  1294→
  1295→  kubectl config use-context "$context"
  1296→
  1297→  # Get a running pod
  1298→  pod_name=$(kubectl get pods -n crewai-${env} -o jsonpath='{.items[0].metadata.name}')
  1299→
  1300→  # Check Cloud SQL Proxy sidecar logs
  1301→  echo "Cloud SQL Proxy logs for $pod_name:"
  1302→  kubectl logs $pod_name -c cloud-sql-proxy -n crewai-${env} | head -5
  1303→
  1304→  echo "---"
  1305→done
  1306→```
  1307→
  1308→### 5.8 Test GitHub Actions Workflows
  1309→
  1310→```bash
  1311→# Verify GitHub Actions workflows are available
  1312→gh workflow list
  1313→
  1314→# View recent workflow runs
  1315→gh run list --limit 5
  1316→
  1317→# If you want to test manually:
  1318→# Create a test branch and PR to trigger workflows
  1319→git checkout -b test/setup-verification
  1320→echo "# Test" >> terraform/root/README.md
  1321→git add terraform/root/README.md
  1322→git commit -m "test: setup verification"
  1323→git push origin test/setup-verification
  1324→gh pr create --title "Test: Setup Verification" --body "Testing CI/CD setup"
  1325→
  1326→# Monitor workflow execution
  1327→gh pr checks
  1328→
  1329→# Clean up after testing
  1330→git checkout main
  1331→git branch -D test/setup-verification
  1332→git push origin --delete test/setup-verification
  1333→gh pr close <pr-number>
  1334→```
  1335→
  1336→### 5.9 Final Health Check Script
  1337→
  1338→```bash
  1339→# Run comprehensive health check
  1340→cat > /tmp/crewai-health-check.sh <<'EOF'
  1341→#!/bin/bash
  1342→
  1343→echo "=== CrewAI Platform Health Check ==="
  1344→echo ""
  1345→
  1346→ENVIRONMENTS=("dev" "qa" "prod")
  1347→PASS=0
  1348→FAIL=0
  1349→
  1350→for env in "${ENVIRONMENTS[@]}"; do
  1351→  echo "Checking $env environment..."
  1352→
  1353→  # GCP Project
  1354→  if gcloud projects describe kd-ix-eur-${env}-gke &>/dev/null; then
  1355→    echo "OK GCP project exists"
  1356→    ((PASS++))
  1357→  else
  1358→    echo "FAIL GCP project missing"
  1359→    ((FAIL++))
  1360→  fi
  1361→
  1362→  # GKE Cluster
  1363→  if gcloud container clusters describe kd-ix-eur-${env}-gke --region=europe-west1 --project=kd-ix-eur-${env}-gke &>/dev/null; then
  1364→    echo "OK GKE cluster running"
  1365→    ((PASS++))
  1366→  else
  1367→    echo "FAIL GKE cluster not accessible"
  1368→    ((FAIL++))
  1369→  fi
  1370→
  1371→  # Cloud SQL
  1372→  if gcloud sql instances describe kd-ix-eur-${env}-cloudsql-platform --project=kd-ix-eur-${env}-platform-data &>/dev/null; then
  1373→    echo "OK Cloud SQL instance running"
  1374→    ((PASS++))
  1375→  else
  1376→    echo "FAIL Cloud SQL instance not accessible"
  1377→    ((FAIL++))
  1378→  fi
  1379→
  1380→  # Kubernetes Namespace
  1381→  case $env in
  1382→    dev) context="gke_kd-ix-eur-dev-gke_europe-west1_kd-ix-eur-dev-gke" ;;
  1383→    qa) context="gke_kd-ix-eur-qa-gke_europe-west1_kd-ix-eur-qa-gke" ;;
  1384→    prod) context="gke_kd-ix-eur-prod-gke_europe-west1_kd-ix-eur-prod-gke" ;;
  1385→  esac
  1386→
  1387→  if kubectl config use-context "$context" &>/dev/null; then
  1388→    if kubectl get namespace crewai-${env} &>/dev/null; then
  1389→      echo "OK Kubernetes namespace exists"
  1390→      ((PASS++))
  1391→    else
  1392→      echo "FAIL Kubernetes namespace not found"
  1393→      ((FAIL++))
  1394→    fi
  1395→  fi
  1396→
  1397→  # ArgoCD Application
  1398→  if kubectl get application crewai-platform-${env} -n argocd &>/dev/null; then
  1399→    status=$(kubectl get application crewai-platform-${env} -n argocd -o jsonpath='{.status.sync.status}')
  1400→    if [ "$status" = "Synced" ]; then
  1401→      echo "OK ArgoCD application synced"
  1402→      ((PASS++))
  1403→    else
  1404→      echo "WARN ArgoCD application status: $status"
  1405→    fi
  1406→  else
  1407→    echo "FAIL ArgoCD application not found"
  1408→    ((FAIL++))
  1409→  fi
  1410→
  1411→  echo ""
  1412→done
  1413→
  1414→echo "=== Summary ==="
  1415→echo "Passed: $PASS"
  1416→echo "Failed: $FAIL"
  1417→
  1418→if [ $FAIL -eq 0 ]; then
  1419→  echo "SUCCESS: All health checks passed!"
  1420→  exit 0
  1421→else
  1422→  echo "WARNING: Some health checks failed. Review above."
  1423→  exit 1
  1424→fi
  1425→EOF
  1426→
  1427→chmod +x /tmp/crewai-health-check.sh
  1428→/tmp/crewai-health-check.sh
  1429→```
  1430→
  1431→---
  1432→
  1433→## Troubleshooting Guide
  1434→
  1435→### GKE Cluster Issues
  1436→
  1437→#### Cluster not accessible
  1438→
  1439→```bash
  1440→# Verify cluster exists
  1441→gcloud container clusters list --project=kd-ix-eur-dev-gke
  1442→
  1443→# Get credentials
  1444→gcloud container clusters get-credentials kd-ix-eur-dev-gke \
  1445→  --region=europe-west1 \
  1446→  --project=kd-ix-eur-dev-gke
  1447→
  1448→# Test connectivity
  1449→kubectl cluster-info
  1450→kubectl get nodes
  1451→```
  1452→
  1453→#### Workload Identity not working
  1454→
  1455→```bash
  1456→# Verify Workload Identity is enabled on cluster
  1457→gcloud container clusters describe kd-ix-eur-dev-gke \
  1458→  --region=europe-west1 \
  1459→  --project=kd-ix-eur-dev-gke \
  1460→  --format="value(workloadIdentityConfig.workloadPool)"
  1461→
  1462→# Expected output: kd-ix-eur-dev-gke.svc.id.goog
  1463→
  1464→# Verify service account binding
  1465→kubectl describe sa crewai-platform-sa -n crewai-dev
  1466→
  1467→# Expected annotation:
  1468→# iam.gke.io/gcp-service-account=crewai-platform-dev@kd-ix-eur-dev-gke.iam.gserviceaccount.com
  1469→```
  1470→
  1471→### Terraform Issues
  1472→
  1473→#### State file corruption
  1474→
  1475→```bash
  1476→# Check state file in GCS
  1477→gsutil ls gs://crewai-terraform-state/
  1478→
  1479→# View state backup
  1480→gsutil version -l gs://crewai-terraform-state/environments/dev/default.tfstate
  1481→
  1482→# Restore from backup if needed
  1483→gsutil cp gs://crewai-terraform-state/environments/dev/default.tfstate-{version} default.tfstate
  1484→terraform state push default.tfstate
  1485→```
  1486→
  1487→#### Module not found
  1488→
  1489→```bash
  1490→# Reinitialize Terraform
  1491→cd terraform/root
  1492→rm -rf .terraform
  1493→terraform init
  1494→
  1495→# Verify all modules are present
  1496→terraform get -update
  1497→```
  1498→
  1499→### External Secrets Issues
  1500→
  1501→#### Secrets not syncing
  1502→
  1503→```bash
  1504→# Check ExternalSecret status
  1505→kubectl describe externalsecret crewai-secrets -n crewai-dev
  1506→
  1507→# Check SecretStore status
  1508→kubectl describe secretstore gcp-secret-manager -n crewai-dev
  1509→
  1510→# Check service account has correct GCP permissions
  1511→gcloud projects get-iam-policy kd-ix-eur-dev-gke \
  1512→  --flatten="bindings[].members" \
  1513→  --filter="bindings.members:crewai-platform-dev@*"
  1514→
  1515→# Trigger manual sync
  1516→kubectl annotate externalsecret crewai-secrets \
  1517→  force-sync=$(date +%s) \
  1518→  -n crewai-dev --overwrite
  1519→
  1520→# Check External Secrets logs
  1521→kubectl logs -n external-secrets-system -l app.kubernetes.io/name=external-secrets
  1522→```
  1523→
  1524→### Gateway API Issues
  1525→
  1526→#### External IP not assigned
  1527→
  1528→```bash
  1529→# Check Gateway status
  1530→kubectl describe gateway crewai-dev-gateway -n crewai-dev
  1531→
  1532→# Check GKE events
  1533→kubectl get events -n crewai-dev --sort-by='.lastTimestamp' | tail -20
  1534→
  1535→# Verify Cloud Armor policy exists
  1536→gcloud compute security-policies describe dev-crewai-security-policy --project=kd-ix-eur-dev-gke
  1537→
  1538→# Check BackendConfig
  1539→kubectl describe backendconfig crewai-dev-backendconfig -n crewai-dev
  1540→
  1541→# Check GCP load balancer status
  1542→gcloud compute forwarding-rules list --filter="name~crewai" --project=kd-ix-eur-dev-gke
  1543→```
  1544→
  1545→#### SSL certificate not provisioning
  1546→
  1547→```bash
  1548→# Check Certificate Manager
  1549→gcloud compute ssl-certificates list --project=kd-ix-eur-dev-gke
  1550→
  1551→# Check certificate status
  1552→gcloud compute ssl-certificates describe crewai-dev-cert \
  1553→  --project=kd-ix-eur-dev-gke \
  1554→  --format="value(managed.domainStatus[].domainName,managed.domainStatus[].status)"
  1555→
  1556→# Verify DNS records point to Gateway IP
  1557→gcloud dns record-sets list --zone=konecta-digital-com
  1558→```
  1559→
  1560→### ArgoCD Issues
  1561→
  1562→#### Application not syncing
  1563→
  1564→```bash
  1565→# Check application status
  1566→kubectl describe application crewai-platform-dev -n argocd
  1567→
  1568→# Check ArgoCD server logs
  1569→kubectl logs -n argocd -l app.kubernetes.io/name=argocd-server
  1570→
  1571→# Manual sync
  1572→kubectl config use-context gke_kd-ix-eur-dev-gke_europe-west1_kd-ix-eur-dev-gke
  1573→argocd app sync crewai-platform-dev
  1574→
  1575→# Refresh application state
  1576→argocd app refresh crewai-platform-dev
  1577→```
  1578→
  1579→#### Git repository connection failed
  1580→
  1581→```bash
  1582→# Check ArgoCD secret
  1583→kubectl get secret argocd-secret -n argocd -o yaml | grep url
  1584→
  1585→# Update repository URL if needed
  1586→argocd repo add https://github.com/your-org/kix-services-crewai.git \
  1587→  --username=<user> \
  1588→  --password=<token>
  1589→
  1590→# List repositories
  1591→argocd repo list
  1592→```
  1593→
  1594→### Network Policy Issues
  1595→
  1596→#### Pods unable to communicate
  1597→
  1598→```bash
  1599→# Check network policies
  1600→kubectl get networkpolicies -n crewai-dev
  1601→
  1602→# Test connectivity between pods
  1603→kubectl run test-pod --image=busybox --restart=Never -n crewai-dev -- sh -c "sleep 3600"
  1604→kubectl exec -it test-pod -n crewai-dev -- wget -q -O- http://crewai-web.crewai-dev.svc.cluster.local:8000/health
  1605→
  1606→# Delete test pod
  1607→kubectl delete pod test-pod -n crewai-dev
  1608→```

  1611→
  1612→## Reference Documentation
  1613→
  1614→- **Terraform**: See `terraform/root/README.md` for detailed Terraform workflow
  1615→- **Kubernetes**: See `kubernetes/kustomize/README.md` for Kustomize structure and customization patterns
  1616→- **ArgoCD Applications**: See actual application files in `argocd/applications/`
  1617→- **Helm Values**: See `helm/values/` for application configuration per environment
  1618→- **CI/CD Workflows**: See `.github/workflows/` for GitHub Actions pipelines
  1619→- **Emergency Procedures**: See `docs/runbooks/` for incident response procedures
  1620→

  1622→
  1623→## Next Steps
  1624→
  1625→1. **Complete Phase 0-5** following the steps above
  1626→2. **Configure DNS** to point to the Gateway API external IPs
  1627→3. **Test application endpoints** once DNS is live
  1628→4. **Set up monitoring** dashboards in Cloud Monitoring
  1629→5. **Configure alerting** in Cloud Monitoring and PagerDuty (optional)
  1630→6. **Schedule team training** on deployment and incident response procedures
  1631→
  1632→---
  1633→
  1634→## Support
  1635→
  1636→For issues or questions:
  1637→
  1638→1. Review this guide's **Troubleshooting** section
  1639→2. Check CI/CD workflow logs: `gh run list`
  1640→3. Check Kubernetes events: `kubectl get events -A`
  1641→4. Review pod logs: `kubectl logs -f <pod> -n <namespace>`
  1642→5. Contact your platform team or create an issue in GitHub
  1643→

</system-reminder>

     1→# CrewAI Platform - Dev Environment
     2→# Composes remote modules from kix-platform-modules + inline resources
     3→
     4→locals {
     5→  labels = {
     6→    app         = "crewai"
     7→    environment = var.environment
     8→    managed-by  = "terraform"
     9→    platform    = "crewai"
    10→  }
    11→
    12→  # Construct SA email statically to avoid for_each unknown value issues
    13→  crewai_sa_email = "crewai-platform-${var.environment}@${var.gke_project_id}.iam.gserviceaccount.com"
    14→}
    15→
    16→# ---------------------------------------------------------------------------
    17→# Providers
    18→# ---------------------------------------------------------------------------
    19→
    20→provider "google" {
    21→  project = var.gke_project_id
    22→  region  = var.region
    23→}
    24→
    25→data "google_client_config" "default" {}
    26→
    27→data "google_container_cluster" "gke" {
    28→  project  = var.gke_project_id
    29→  name     = var.gke_cluster_name
    30→  location = var.region
    31→}
    32→
    33→provider "kubernetes" {
    34→  host                   = "https://${data.google_container_cluster.gke.endpoint}"
    35→  token                  = data.google_client_config.default.access_token
    36→  cluster_ca_certificate = base64decode(data.google_container_cluster.gke.master_auth[0].cluster_ca_certificate)
    37→}
    38→
    39→# ---------------------------------------------------------------------------
    40→# Data: Existing Cloud SQL Instance
    41→# ---------------------------------------------------------------------------
    42→
    43→data "google_sql_database_instance" "platform" {
    44→  project = var.platform_data_project
    45→  name    = var.cloud_sql_instance_name
    46→}
    47→
    48→# ---------------------------------------------------------------------------
    49→# Cloud SQL: Databases + User on Existing Instance
    50→# ---------------------------------------------------------------------------
    51→
    52→resource "google_sql_database" "production" {
    53→  provider = google
    54→  project  = var.platform_data_project
    55→  instance = data.google_sql_database_instance.platform.name
    56→  name     = "crewai_plus_production"
    57→}
    58→
    59→resource "google_sql_database" "cable" {
    60→  provider = google
    61→  project  = var.platform_data_project
    62→  instance = data.google_sql_database_instance.platform.name
    63→  name     = "crewai_plus_cable_production"
    64→}
    65→
    66→resource "google_sql_database" "oauth" {
    67→  provider = google
    68→  project  = var.platform_data_project
    69→  instance = data.google_sql_database_instance.platform.name
    70→  name     = "crewai_plus_oauth_db"
    71→}
    72→
    73→resource "random_password" "db_password" {
    74→  length  = 32
    75→  special = false
    76→}
    77→
    78→resource "random_password" "secret_key_base" {
    79→  length  = 128
    80→  special = true
    81→}
    82→
    83→resource "random_password" "platform_master_key" {
    84→  length  = 32
    85→  special = false
    86→}
    87→
    88→resource "google_sql_user" "crewai" {
    89→  provider = google
    90→  project  = var.platform_data_project
    91→  instance = data.google_sql_database_instance.platform.name
    92→  name     = "crewai"
    93→  password = random_password.db_password.result
    94→}
    95→
    96→# ---------------------------------------------------------------------------
    97→# GCS: Data + Logs Buckets (remote module)
    98→# ---------------------------------------------------------------------------
    99→
   100→module "gcs_data" {
   101→  source = "github.com/konecta-ix-platform/kix-platform-modules//terraform/gcp/data-services/gcs?ref=main"
   102→
   103→  project_id  = var.gke_project_id
   104→  environment = var.environment
   105→  use_case    = "crewai"
   106→  name_suffix = "data"
   107→  cost_center = "platform"
   108→  location    = var.region
   109→}
   110→
   111→module "gcs_logs" {
   112→  source = "github.com/konecta-ix-platform/kix-platform-modules//terraform/gcp/data-services/gcs?ref=main"
   113→
   114→  project_id  = var.gke_project_id
   115→  environment = var.environment
   116→  use_case    = "crewai"
   117→  name_suffix = "logs"
   118→  cost_center = "platform"
   119→  location    = var.region
   120→}
   121→
   122→# ---------------------------------------------------------------------------
   123→# GCP Service Account (inline to avoid circular deps with HMAC/secrets)
   124→# ---------------------------------------------------------------------------
   125→
   126→resource "google_service_account" "crewai_platform" {
   127→  project      = var.gke_project_id
   128→  account_id   = "crewai-platform-${var.environment}"
   129→  display_name = "CrewAI Platform - ${var.environment}"
   130→}
   131→
   132→# ---------------------------------------------------------------------------
   133→# GCS: HMAC Keys for S3-Compatible Access
   134→# ---------------------------------------------------------------------------
   135→
   136→resource "google_storage_hmac_key" "crewai" {
   137→  project               = var.gke_project_id
   138→  service_account_email = google_service_account.crewai_platform.email
   139→}
   140→
   141→# ---------------------------------------------------------------------------
   142→# Secret Manager: Application Secrets (one module call per credential)
   143→# ---------------------------------------------------------------------------
   144→
   145→module "secret_db_credentials" {
   146→  source = "github.com/konecta-ix-platform/kix-platform-modules//terraform/gcp/secret-manager?ref=main"
   147→
   148→  project_id  = var.security_project_id
   149→  environment = var.environment
   150→  secret_id   = "crewai-db-credentials-${var.environment}"
   151→  secret_data = jsonencode({
   152→    password = random_password.db_password.result
   153→    host     = data.google_sql_database_instance.platform.private_ip_address
   154→    port     = 5432
   155→    database = "crewai_plus_production"
   156→    username = "crewai"
   157→  })
   158→
   159→  accessor_service_accounts = [local.crewai_sa_email]
   160→  additional_labels         = local.labels
   161→}
   162→
   163→module "secret_gcs_credentials" {
   164→  source = "github.com/konecta-ix-platform/kix-platform-modules//terraform/gcp/secret-manager?ref=main"
   165→
   166→  project_id  = var.security_project_id
   167→  environment = var.environment
   168→  secret_id   = "crewai-gcs-credentials-${var.environment}"
   169→  secret_data = jsonencode({
   170→    access_key_id     = google_storage_hmac_key.crewai.access_id
   171→    secret_access_key = google_storage_hmac_key.crewai.secret
   172→  })
   173→
   174→  accessor_service_accounts = [local.crewai_sa_email]
   175→  additional_labels         = local.labels
   176→}
   177→
   178→module "secret_oauth_credentials" {
   179→  source = "github.com/konecta-ix-platform/kix-platform-modules//terraform/gcp/secret-manager?ref=main"
   180→
   181→  project_id  = var.security_project_id
   182→  environment = var.environment
   183→  secret_id   = "crewai-oauth-credentials-${var.environment}"
   184→  secret_data = jsonencode({
   185→    provider  = "workos"
   186→    client_id = var.oauth_client_id
   187→    api_key   = var.oauth_api_key
   188→    domain    = var.oauth_domain
   189→  })
   190→
   191→  accessor_service_accounts = [local.crewai_sa_email]
   192→  additional_labels         = local.labels
   193→}
   194→
   195→module "secret_helm_registry" {
   196→  source = "github.com/konecta-ix-platform/kix-platform-modules//terraform/gcp/secret-manager?ref=main"
   197→
   198→  project_id  = var.security_project_id
   199→  environment = var.environment
   200→  secret_id   = "crewai-helm-registry-token-${var.environment}"
   201→  secret_data = var.helm_registry_token
   202→
   203→  accessor_service_accounts = [local.crewai_sa_email]
   204→  additional_labels         = local.labels
   205→}
   206→
   207→# ---------------------------------------------------------------------------
   208→# IAM Bindings for Service Account
   209→# ---------------------------------------------------------------------------
   210→
   211→resource "google_project_iam_member" "cloudsql_client" {
   212→  project = var.platform_data_project
   213→  role    = "roles/cloudsql.client"
   214→  member  = "serviceAccount:${google_service_account.crewai_platform.email}"
   215→}
   216→
   217→resource "google_storage_bucket_iam_member" "data_bucket" {
   218→  bucket = module.gcs_data.bucket_name
   219→  role   = "roles/storage.objectAdmin"
   220→  member = "serviceAccount:${google_service_account.crewai_platform.email}"
   221→}
   222→
   223→resource "google_storage_bucket_iam_member" "logs_bucket" {
   224→  bucket = module.gcs_logs.bucket_name
   225→  role   = "roles/storage.objectCreator"
   226→  member = "serviceAccount:${google_service_account.crewai_platform.email}"
   227→}
   228→
   229→resource "google_service_account_iam_member" "workload_identity" {
   230→  service_account_id = google_service_account.crewai_platform.name
   231→  role               = "roles/iam.workloadIdentityUser"
   232→  member             = "serviceAccount:${var.gke_project_id}.svc.id.goog[crewai-${var.environment}/crewai-${var.environment}-sa]"
   233→}
   234→
   235→resource "google_service_account_iam_member" "github_impersonation" {
   236→  service_account_id = google_service_account.crewai_platform.name
   237→  role               = "roles/iam.serviceAccountTokenCreator"
   238→  member             = "serviceAccount:github-kix-services-crewai-${var.environment}@${var.gke_project_id}.iam.gserviceaccount.com"
   239→}
   240→
   241→# ---------------------------------------------------------------------------
   242→# Artifact Registry: For CrewAI Builder (crew container images)
   243→# ---------------------------------------------------------------------------
   244→
   245→resource "google_artifact_registry_repository" "crewai_builder" {
   246→  project       = var.gke_project_id
   247→  location      = var.region
   248→  repository_id = "crewai-${var.environment}"
   249→  description   = "Container registry for CrewAI crew builds"
   250→  format        = "DOCKER"
   251→
   252→  labels = local.labels
   253→}
   254→
   255→resource "google_artifact_registry_repository_iam_member" "builder_writer" {
   256→  project    = var.gke_project_id
   257→  location   = var.region
   258→  repository = google_artifact_registry_repository.crewai_builder.name
   259→  role       = "roles/artifactregistry.writer"
   260→  member     = "serviceAccount:${google_service_account.crewai_platform.email}"
   261→}
   262→
   263→# ---------------------------------------------------------------------------
   264→# Kubernetes: Namespaces (inline - avoids gke-config module's default namespaces)
   265→# ---------------------------------------------------------------------------
   266→
   267→resource "kubernetes_namespace" "crewai_platform" {
   268→  metadata {
   269→    name = "crewai-${var.environment}"
   270→    labels = merge(local.labels, {
   271→      "app.kubernetes.io/name" = "crewai-platform"
   272→    })
   273→  }
   274→}
   275→
   276→resource "kubernetes_namespace" "crewai_crews" {
   277→  metadata {
   278→    name = "crewai-crews-${var.environment}"
   279→    labels = merge(local.labels, {
   280→      "app.kubernetes.io/name" = "crewai-crews"
   281→    })
   282→  }
   283→}
   284→
   285→# ---------------------------------------------------------------------------
   286→# Kubernetes: Service Accounts with Workload Identity
   287→# ---------------------------------------------------------------------------
   288→
   289→resource "kubernetes_service_account" "crewai_platform" {
   290→  metadata {
   291→    name      = "crewai-${var.environment}-sa"
   292→    namespace = kubernetes_namespace.crewai_platform.metadata[0].name
   293→    labels    = local.labels
   294→    annotations = {
   295→      "iam.gke.io/gcp-service-account" = google_service_account.crewai_platform.email
   296→    }
   297→  }
   298→  automount_service_account_token = false
   299→}
   300→
   301→resource "kubernetes_service_account" "crewai_crews" {
   302→  metadata {
   303→    name      = "crewai-crews-sa"
   304→    namespace = kubernetes_namespace.crewai_crews.metadata[0].name
   305→    labels    = local.labels
   306→  }
   307→  automount_service_account_token = false
   308→}
   309→
   310→# ---------------------------------------------------------------------------
   311→# Kubernetes: Limit Ranges (defaults for pods without explicit limits)
   312→# ---------------------------------------------------------------------------
   313→
   314→resource "kubernetes_limit_range" "crewai_platform" {
   315→  metadata {
   316→    name      = "crewai-platform-limits"
   317→    namespace = kubernetes_namespace.crewai_platform.metadata[0].name
   318→    labels    = local.labels
   319→  }
   320→  spec {
   321→    limit {
   322→      type = "Container"
   323→      default = {
   324→        cpu    = "500m"
   325→        memory = "512Mi"
   326→      }
   327→      default_request = {
   328→        cpu    = "100m"
   329→        memory = "128Mi"
   330→      }
   331→    }
   332→  }
   333→}
   334→
   335→resource "kubernetes_limit_range" "crewai_crews" {
   336→  metadata {
   337→    name      = "crewai-crews-limits"
   338→    namespace = kubernetes_namespace.crewai_crews.metadata[0].name
   339→    labels    = local.labels
   340→  }
   341→  spec {
   342→    limit {
   343→      type = "Container"
   344→      default = {
   345→        cpu    = "1"
   346→        memory = "1Gi"
   347→      }
   348→      default_request = {
   349→        cpu    = "250m"
   350→        memory = "256Mi"
   351→      }
   352→    }
   353→  }
   354→}
   355→
   356→# ---------------------------------------------------------------------------
   357→# Kubernetes: Resource Quotas
   358→# ---------------------------------------------------------------------------
   359→
   360→resource "kubernetes_resource_quota" "crewai_platform" {
   361→  metadata {
   362→    name      = "crewai-platform-quota"
   363→    namespace = kubernetes_namespace.crewai_platform.metadata[0].name
   364→    labels    = local.labels
   365→  }
   366→  spec {
   367→    hard = {
   368→      "requests.cpu"    = "20"
   369→      "requests.memory" = "40Gi"
   370→      "limits.cpu"      = "40"
   371→      "limits.memory"   = "80Gi"
   372→      pods              = "50"
   373→      services          = "20"
   374→      secrets           = "30"
   375→      configmaps        = "30"
   376→      persistentvolumeclaims = "10"
   377→    }
   378→  }
   379→  depends_on = [kubernetes_limit_range.crewai_platform]
   380→}
   381→
   382→resource "kubernetes_resource_quota" "crewai_crews" {
   383→  metadata {
   384→    name      = "crewai-crews-quota"
   385→    namespace = kubernetes_namespace.crewai_crews.metadata[0].name
   386→    labels    = local.labels
   387→  }
   388→  spec {
   389→    hard = {
   390→      "requests.cpu"    = "40"
   391→      "requests.memory" = "80Gi"
   392→      "limits.cpu"      = "80"
   393→      "limits.memory"   = "160Gi"
   394→      pods              = "100"
   395→      services          = "10"
   396→      secrets           = "20"
   397→      configmaps        = "20"
   398→      persistentvolumeclaims = "10"
   399→    }
   400→  }
   401→  depends_on = [kubernetes_limit_range.crewai_crews]
   402→}
   403→
   404→# ---------------------------------------------------------------------------
   405→# K8s Secrets: Application Credentials
   406→# ---------------------------------------------------------------------------
   407→
   408→resource "kubernetes_secret" "crewai_credentials" {
   409→  metadata {
   410→    name      = "crewai-credentials"
   411→    namespace = kubernetes_namespace.crewai_platform.metadata[0].name
   412→    labels    = local.labels
   413→  }
   414→
   415→  data = {
   416→    DB_PASSWORD           = random_password.db_password.result
   417→    SECRET_KEY_BASE       = random_password.secret_key_base.result
   418→    PLATFORM_MASTER_KEY   = random_password.platform_master_key.result
   419→    AWS_ACCESS_KEY_ID     = google_storage_hmac_key.crewai.access_id
   420→    AWS_SECRET_ACCESS_KEY = google_storage_hmac_key.crewai.secret
   421→    WORKOS_CLIENT_ID      = var.oauth_client_id
   422→    WORKOS_API_KEY        = var.oauth_api_key
   423→  }
   424→}
   425→
   426→resource "kubernetes_secret" "helm_registry" {
   427→  metadata {
   428→    name      = "crewai-registry-secret"
   429→    namespace = kubernetes_namespace.crewai_platform.metadata[0].name
   430→    labels    = local.labels
   431→  }
   432→
   433→  type = "kubernetes.io/dockerconfigjson"
   434→
   435→  data = {
   436→    ".dockerconfigjson" = jsonencode({
   437→      auths = {
   438→        "registry.crewai.com" = {
   439→          auth = var.helm_registry_token
   440→        }
   441→      }
   442→    })
   443→  }
   444→}
   445→
   446→resource "kubernetes_secret" "artifact_registry" {
   447→  metadata {
   448→    name      = "artifact-registry-secret"
   449→    namespace = kubernetes_namespace.crewai_platform.metadata[0].name
   450→    labels    = local.labels
   451→  }
   452→
   453→  type = "kubernetes.io/dockerconfigjson"
   454→
   455→  data = {
   456→    ".dockerconfigjson" = jsonencode({
   457→      auths = {
   458→        "${var.region}-docker.pkg.dev" = {
   459→          username = "_json_key_base64"
   460→          password = var.artifact_registry_key
   461→        }
   462→      }
   463→    })
   464→  }
   465→}
   466→
   467→# ---------------------------------------------------------------------------
   468→# K8s ConfigMap: Database Configuration (required by CrewAI chart)
   469→# ---------------------------------------------------------------------------
   470→
   471→resource "kubernetes_config_map" "database_config" {
   472→  metadata {
   473→    name      = "crewai-${var.environment}-database-config"
   474→    namespace = kubernetes_namespace.crewai_platform.metadata[0].name
   475→    labels    = local.labels
   476→  }
   477→
   478→  data = {
   479→    "database.yml" = yamlencode({
   480→      production = {
   481→        adapter  = "postgresql"
   482→        host     = "cloud-sql-proxy"
   483→        port     = 5432
   484→        database = "crewai_plus_production"
   485→        username = "crewai"
   486→        pool     = 5
   487→      }
   488→      cable = {
   489→        adapter  = "postgresql"
   490→        host     = "cloud-sql-proxy"
   491→        port     = 5432
   492→        database = "crewai_plus_cable_production"
   493→        username = "crewai"
   494→        pool     = 5
   495→      }
   496→    })
   497→  }
   498→}
   499→
   500→# ---------------------------------------------------------------------------
   501→# Cloud SQL Proxy: Standalone Deployment for shared DB access
   502→# ---------------------------------------------------------------------------
   503→
   504→resource "kubernetes_deployment" "cloud_sql_proxy" {
   505→  metadata {
   506→    name      = "cloud-sql-proxy"
   507→    namespace = kubernetes_namespace.crewai_platform.metadata[0].name
   508→    labels    = merge(local.labels, { component = "cloud-sql-proxy" })
   509→  }
   510→
   511→  spec {
   512→    replicas = 1
   513→
   514→    selector {
   515→      match_labels = {
   516→        app       = "cloud-sql-proxy"
   517→        component = "cloud-sql-proxy"
   518→      }
   519→    }
   520→
   521→    template {
   522→      metadata {
   523→        labels = {
   524→          app       = "cloud-sql-proxy"
   525→          component = "cloud-sql-proxy"
   526→        }
   527→      }
   528→
   529→      spec {
   530→        service_account_name = kubernetes_service_account.crewai_platform.metadata[0].name
   531→
   532→        container {
   533→          name  = "cloud-sql-proxy"
   534→          image = "gcr.io/cloud-sql-connectors/cloud-sql-proxy:2.8.2"
   535→
   536→          args = [
   537→            "--structured-logs",
   538→            "--private-ip",
   539→            "--address=0.0.0.0",
   540→            "--port=5432",
   541→            "--health-check",
   542→            "--http-port=9090",
   543→            "--http-address=0.0.0.0",
   544→            "${var.platform_data_project}:${var.region}:${var.cloud_sql_instance_name}"
   545→          ]
   546→
   547→          port {
   548→            container_port = 5432
   549→            name           = "postgres"
   550→          }
   551→
   552→          port {
   553→            container_port = 9090
   554→            name           = "http"
   555→          }
   556→
   557→          resources {
   558→            requests = {
   559→              cpu    = "100m"
   560→              memory = "128Mi"
   561→            }
   562→            limits = {
   563→              cpu    = "500m"
   564→              memory = "512Mi"
   565→            }
   566→          }
   567→
   568→          security_context {
   569→            run_as_non_root            = true
   570→            run_as_user                = 65532
   571→            allow_privilege_escalation = false
   572→          }
   573→
   574→          liveness_probe {
   575→            http_get {
   576→              path = "/liveness"
   577→              port = 9090
   578→            }
   579→            initial_delay_seconds = 30
   580→            period_seconds        = 10
   581→          }
   582→
   583→          readiness_probe {
   584→            http_get {
   585→              path = "/readiness"
   586→              port = 9090
   587→            }
   588→            initial_delay_seconds = 10
   589→            period_seconds        = 5
   590→          }
   591→        }
   592→      }
   593→    }
   594→  }
   595→}
   596→
   597→resource "kubernetes_service" "cloud_sql_proxy" {
   598→  metadata {
   599→    name      = "cloud-sql-proxy"
   600→    namespace = kubernetes_namespace.crewai_platform.metadata[0].name
   601→    labels    = merge(local.labels, { component = "cloud-sql-proxy" })
   602→  }
   603→
   604→  spec {
   605→    selector = {
   606→      app       = "cloud-sql-proxy"
   607→      component = "cloud-sql-proxy"
   608→    }
   609→
   610→    port {
   611→      port        = 5432
   612→      target_port = 5432
   613→      name        = "postgres"
   614→    }
   615→
   616→    type = "ClusterIP"
   617→  }
   618→}
   619→

{
  "replace_all": false,
  "file_path": "/home/nathan_booth/code/konecta-ix-services/kix-services-crewai/environments/dev/terraform/main.tf",
  "old_string": "resource \"kubernetes_secret\" \"artifact_registry\" {\n  metadata {\n    name      = \"artifact-registry-secret\"\n    namespace = kubernetes_namespace.crewai_platform.metadata[0].name\n    labels    = local.labels\n  }\n\n  type = \"kubernetes.io/dockerconfigjson\"\n\n  data = {\n    \".dockerconfigjson\" = jsonencode({\n      auths = {\n        \"${var.region}-docker.pkg.dev\" = {\n          username = \"_json_key_base64\"\n          password = var.artifact_registry_key\n        }\n      }\n    })\n  }\n}",
  "new_string": "# ---------------------------------------------------------------------------\n# Artifact Registry: Service Account Key for Image Pulling (synced via ESO)\n# ---------------------------------------------------------------------------\n\n# Create a service account key for AR access (used by pods via ESO)\nresource \"google_service_account_key\" \"ar_reader\" {\n  service_account_id = google_service_account.crewai_platform.name\n}\n\n# Store the SA key in Secret Manager (ESO will sync to K8s)\nmodule \"secret_ar_credentials\" {\n  source = \"github.com/konecta-ix-platform/kix-platform-modules//terraform/gcp/secret-manager?ref=main\"\n\n  project_id  = var.security_project_id\n  environment = var.environment\n  secret_id   = \"crewai-ar-credentials-${var.environment}\"\n  secret_data = base64decode(google_service_account_key.ar_reader.private_key)\n\n  # ESO SA needs access to read this secret\n  accessor_service_accounts = [\n    local.crewai_sa_email,\n    \"external-secrets-${var.environment}@${var.gke_project_id}.iam.gserviceaccount.com\"\n  ]\n  additional_labels = local.labels\n}\n\n# Grant AR reader role to the platform SA (for pulling images)\nresource \"google_artifact_registry_repository_iam_member\" \"platform_reader\" {\n  project    = var.gke_project_id\n  location   = var.region\n  repository = google_artifact_registry_repository.crewai_builder.name\n  role       = \"roles/artifactregistry.reader\"\n  member     = \"serviceAccount:${google_service_account.crewai_platform.email}\"\n}"
}

Now let me add the ESO service account with Workload Identity binding.

> TOOL

tool_use Edit
id: toolu_0192QGhVKHC9Cn1XpvqGPShN
```json
{
  "replace_all": false,
  "file_path": "/home/nathan_booth/code/konecta-ix-services/kix-services-crewai/environments/dev/terraform/main.tf",
  "old_string": "# Grant AR reader role to the platform SA (for pulling images)\nresource \"google_artifact_registry_repository_iam_member\" \"platform_reader\" {\n  project    = var.gke_project_id\n  location   = var.region\n  repository = google_artifact_registry_repository.crewai_builder.name\n  role       = \"roles/artifactregistry.reader\"\n  member     = \"serviceAccount:${google_service_account.crewai_platform.email}\"\n}",
  "new_string": "# Grant AR reader role to the platform SA (for pulling images)\nresource \"google_artifact_registry_repository_iam_member\" \"platform_reader\" {\n  project    = var.gke_project_id\n  location   = var.region\n  repository = google_artifact_registry_repository.crewai_builder.name\n  role       = \"roles/artifactregistry.reader\"\n  member     = \"serviceAccount:${google_service_account.crewai_platform.email}\"\n}\n\n# ---------------------------------------------------------------------------\n# External Secrets Operator: GCP Service Account with Workload Identity\n# ---------------------------------------------------------------------------\n\nresource \"google_service_account\" \"external_secrets\" {\n  project      = var.gke_project_id\n  account_id   = \"external-secrets-${var.environment}\"\n  display_name = \"External Secrets Operator - ${var.environment}\"\n}\n\n# Workload Identity binding for ESO\nresource \"google_service_account_iam_member\" \"eso_workload_identity\" {\n  service_account_id = google_service_account.external_secrets.name\n  role               = \"roles/iam.workloadIdentityUser\"\n  member             = \"serviceAccount:${var.gke_project_id}.svc.id.goog[external-secrets/external-secrets]\"\n}\n\n# Grant ESO SA permission to access Secret Manager secrets\nresource \"google_project_iam_member\" \"eso_secret_accessor\" {\n  project = var.security_project_id\n  role    = \"roles/secretmanager.secretAccessor\"\n  member  = \"serviceAccount:${google_service_account.external_secrets.email}\"\n}"
}
```
