# Security Audit Report: CrewAI Deployment Repository

**Repository:** kix-services-crewai  
**Audit Date:** 2026-02-06  
**Auditor:** Security Auditor Agent  
**Scope:** Multi-environment deployment infrastructure for CrewAI Enterprise on GKE

---

## Executive Summary

This security audit reviewed the CrewAI deployment repository covering Terraform IAM configurations, secret management, Workload Identity, network security, Pod Security Standards, ArgoCD configurations, CI/CD pipeline security, and access controls. The audit identified **8 findings** across different risk levels, with the majority being low to medium risk with actionable remediations.

**Overall Security Posture:** GOOD - The repository demonstrates solid security practices including Workload Identity Federation, proper secret management via GCP Secret Manager, security scanning in CI/CD, and defense-in-depth controls. Key areas for improvement include dev environment hardening and network policy enforcement.

---

## Findings Summary

| Risk Level | Count | Description |
|------------|-------|-------------|
| Critical | 0 | None identified |
| High | 1 | Missing security controls in dev environment |
| Medium | 3 | Pod security gaps, network policy defaults, ArgoCD exposure |
| Low | 4 | Configuration improvements, documentation gaps |

---

## Detailed Findings

### HIGH RISK FINDINGS

#### H-1: Dev Environment Missing Pod Security Context and Probes

**File:** `/home/nathan_booth/code/konecta-ix-services/kix-services-crewai/environments/dev/helm/crewai/values.yaml`

**Description:** The dev environment has commented-out security contexts, liveness probes, readiness probes, and startup probes for web, worker, and buildkit components (lines 48-97, 117-155, 169-193). While QA and prod environments have these enabled, the dev environment runs without:
- `runAsNonRoot: true`
- `allowPrivilegeEscalation: false`
- `capabilities.drop: ALL`
- `seccompProfile: RuntimeDefault`
- Health probes for pod lifecycle management

**Risk:** Pods in dev may run as root, allow privilege escalation, and lack proper container isolation. This creates attack surface and violates least-privilege principles.

**Remediation:**
1. Uncomment the podSecurityContext and securityContext blocks in dev/helm/crewai/values.yaml
2. Enable liveness, readiness, and startup probes for all components
3. Ensure parity between dev and prod security configurations

---

### MEDIUM RISK FINDINGS

#### M-1: Network Policy Conditionally Enabled

**File:** `/home/nathan_booth/code/konecta-ix-services/kix-services-crewai/environments/dev/helm/crewai/templates/networkpolicy.yaml`

**Description:** Network policies are template-controlled via `.Values.networkPolicy.enabled`. If not explicitly set to true, no network segmentation is applied. The network policy template exists but requires explicit enablement.

**Risk:** Without network policies, all pods can communicate freely within the cluster, enabling lateral movement if a pod is compromised.

**Remediation:**
1. Add `networkPolicy.enabled: true` to all environment values.yaml files
2. Enable egress controls with `networkPolicy.egress.enabled: true`
3. Configure `networkPolicy.isolateEnvironments: true` to prevent cross-environment traffic

---

#### M-2: Cloud Armor Disabled in Dev Environment

**File:** `/home/nathan_booth/code/konecta-ix-services/kix-services-crewai/environments/dev/helm/crewai/values-gke.yaml`

**Description:** Cloud Armor WAF protection is disabled for dev (line 59: `cloudArmor.enabled: false`).

**Risk:** Dev environment is exposed to web application attacks including OWASP Top 10 threats (SQLi, XSS, etc.).

**Remediation:**
1. Enable Cloud Armor in dev for security testing parity: `cloudArmor.enabled: true`
2. Apply preconfigured WAF rules for OWASP protection
3. Validate WAF rules don't block legitimate dev traffic

---

#### M-3: ArgoCD Application Syncs to Hardcoded IP Address in Dev

**File:** `/home/nathan_booth/code/konecta-ix-services/kix-services-crewai/environments/dev/argocd/apps/application.yaml`

**Description:** Line 37 contains a hardcoded IP address for the destination server: `server: https://34.78.246.181`. This differs from QA/prod which use `https://kubernetes.default.svc`.

**Risk:** 
- Hardcoded IPs can become stale if cluster is recreated
- External IP exposure may bypass internal service mesh routing
- Inconsistent pattern between environments

**Remediation:**
1. Change dev destination to `server: https://kubernetes.default.svc` for consistency
2. If external access is required, use DNS names instead of IPs
3. Document the reason if deviation is intentional

---

### LOW RISK FINDINGS

#### L-1: Terraform Outputs Expose Sensitive Data

**File:** `/home/nathan_booth/code/konecta-ix-services/kix-services-crewai/environments/dev/terraform/outputs.tf`

**Description:** While marked as `sensitive = true`, the outputs still expose HMAC access ID (line 28) and database password (line 22). Anyone with terraform state access can retrieve these.

**Risk:** State file access allows credential extraction. Terraform state is stored in GCS but access controls depend on bucket IAM.

**Remediation:**
1. Avoid outputting credentials unless absolutely necessary for downstream automation
2. Consider removing `hmac_access_id` and `database_password` outputs
3. Ensure GCS state bucket has restricted IAM bindings (verified: bucket `kd-tfstate-services-dev`)

---

#### L-2: BuildKit Requires Elevated Capabilities

**Files:** 
- `/home/nathan_booth/code/konecta-ix-services/kix-services-crewai/environments/qa/helm/values.yaml` (lines 178-186)
- `/home/nathan_booth/code/konecta-ix-services/kix-services-crewai/environments/prod/helm/values.yaml` (lines 218-226)

**Description:** BuildKit component requires Linux capabilities: CHOWN, DAC_OVERRIDE, FOWNER, SETGID, SETUID. This is documented as a known requirement for BuildKit operation.

**Risk:** These capabilities expand container privileges beyond minimal. While necessary for BuildKit functionality, they represent an elevated attack surface.

**Remediation:**
1. Document BuildKit capability requirements in security documentation
2. Ensure BuildKit pods are isolated via network policies
3. Consider running BuildKit in a dedicated node pool with taints/tolerations
4. Monitor BuildKit pods for anomalous behavior

---

#### L-3: Default ARGOCD Project References in Documentation

**Files:** `/home/nathan_booth/code/konecta-ix-services/kix-services-crewai/docs/prps/crewai-gcp-deployment.md`

**Description:** Documentation examples show `project: default` (lines 2317, 2375, 2459) while actual deployments correctly use `project: platform`.

**Risk:** Developers might copy documentation examples and deploy to the default project which may have broader permissions.

**Remediation:**
1. Update documentation to show `project: platform` consistently
2. Add a note warning against using `project: default`

---

#### L-4: GitHub Workflow Token Expiration Not Configured

**File:** `/home/nathan_booth/code/konecta-ix-services/kix-services-crewai/.github/workflows/terraform-apply.yml`

**Description:** The GitHub App token generated for private module access (lines 85-92) doesn't explicitly configure token expiration or scope limitations.

**Risk:** Tokens may have longer than necessary lifetime. Default is typically 1 hour which is reasonable.

**Remediation:**
1. Consider adding explicit token expiration configuration if supported
2. Scope repository access to minimum required (`kix-platform-modules` only - already done correctly)

---

## Positive Security Controls Identified

### Strong Controls in Place

1. **Workload Identity Federation:** GCP service accounts use Workload Identity binding, eliminating the need for service account keys (lines 217-221 in main.tf).

2. **Secret Management:** All secrets stored in GCP Secret Manager with IAM-based access controls and accessor service account restrictions.

3. **Kubernetes Service Account Token Automounting Disabled:** Both platform and crews service accounts have `automount_service_account_token = false` (lines 286, 295 in main.tf).

4. **Security Scanning in CI/CD:** Trivy security scanning enabled in terraform-validate workflow with severity tracking.

5. **Container Image Signing:** Cosign keyless signing implemented for container images with verification.

6. **Least Privilege IAM:** Service accounts have targeted role bindings:
   - `roles/cloudsql.client` for database access
   - `roles/storage.objectAdmin` for data bucket (could be more restrictive)
   - `roles/storage.objectCreator` for logs bucket (write-only)
   - `roles/artifactregistry.reader/writer` scoped to specific repositories

7. **Resource Quotas and Limit Ranges:** Both namespaces have resource quotas and limit ranges configured.

8. **Separate Namespace for Crew Workloads:** Platform services isolated from crew execution (`crewai-{env}` vs `crewai-crews-{env}`).

9. **Pod Disruption Budgets:** Configured for production workloads ensuring availability.

10. **HTTPS Everywhere:** TLS enabled for all web components with auto-generated certificates.

11. **Cloud SQL Proxy Security:** Runs as non-root user (65532) with privilege escalation disabled and all capabilities dropped.

12. **Production ArgoCD Manual Sync:** Production ArgoCD application requires manual sync (no `automated:` block in prod application.yaml).

---

## Remediation Roadmap

### Immediate Actions (0-7 days)
1. **H-1:** Enable security contexts in dev environment
2. **M-3:** Fix hardcoded IP in dev ArgoCD application

### Short-Term Actions (7-30 days)
1. **M-1:** Enable network policies across all environments
2. **M-2:** Enable Cloud Armor for dev environment
3. **L-1:** Review necessity of sensitive outputs

### Ongoing Actions
1. **L-2:** Monitor BuildKit security posture
2. **L-3:** Update documentation examples
3. **L-4:** Review GitHub token configurations periodically

---

## Recommendations for Enhanced Security

1. **Implement Pod Security Admission:** Consider enforcing `restricted` PSS for non-BuildKit workloads using namespace labels.

2. **Add Resource Quota for Secrets:** The quota allows 30 secrets per namespace; consider if this is appropriate.

3. **Enable GKE Binary Authorization:** Enforce that only signed images can be deployed.

4. **Configure VPC Service Controls:** Add CrewAI services to a VPC-SC perimeter for data exfiltration prevention.

5. **Implement Falco or Similar Runtime Security:** Add runtime threat detection for container workloads.

6. **Regular Secret Rotation:** Implement automated rotation for database passwords and HMAC keys.

---

## Conclusion

The CrewAI deployment repository demonstrates a mature security posture with strong controls for identity management, secrets, and CI/CD security. The primary gaps are in dev environment parity and network policy enforcement. Implementing the recommended remediations will elevate the security posture from GOOD to EXCELLENT.

**Next Audit Recommended:** 90 days from this report to validate remediation completion.

# Terraform Code Review Report
## kix-services-crewai Repository

**Review Date:** February 6, 2026  
**Environments Reviewed:** dev, qa, prod  
**Module Source:** github.com/konecta-ix-platform/kix-platform-modules

---

## Executive Summary

The Terraform infrastructure demonstrates **strong foundational practices** with well-structured module composition and good security patterns. However, there are **critical inconsistencies** between environments and several opportunities for DRY improvements. The dev environment has significantly diverged from qa/prod, creating maintainability risks and potential production issues.

**Overall Assessment:** 7/10 (Good foundation, needs standardization)

---

## Critical Issues

### 1. **Environment Divergence - Dev vs QA/Prod**

**Severity:** CRITICAL  
**Risk:** High likelihood of dev/prod parity failures

**Issue:**
- Dev environment uses **inline Kubernetes resources** (namespaces, service accounts, limit ranges, resource quotas, Cloud SQL proxy deployment)
- QA/Prod environments use **gke-config module** for the same resources
- This violates the "identical infrastructure across environments" principle

**Files Affected:**
- `/home/nathan_booth/code/konecta-ix-services/kix-services-crewai/environments/dev/terraform/main.tf` (lines 255-578)
- `/home/nathan_booth/code/konecta-ix-services/kix-services-crewai/environments/qa/terraform/main.tf` (lines 210-289)
- `/home/nathan_booth/code/konecta-ix-services/kix-services-crewai/environments/prod/terraform/main.tf` (lines 210-296)

**Impact:**
- Configuration drift between environments
- Different resource management patterns (inline vs module)
- Dev missing network isolation policies that QA/Prod have
- Dev deployments (Cloud SQL proxy) not tracked in QA/Prod
- Dev service account naming differs: `crewai-{env}-sa` vs QA/Prod: `crewai-platform-sa`

**Recommendation:**
Refactor dev to use the gke-config module like QA/Prod. Move the Cloud SQL proxy deployment to a separate module or add it to QA/Prod as well.

---

### 2. **Kubernetes Provider Configuration Inconsistency**

**Severity:** HIGH  
**Risk:** Authentication failures, plan/apply issues

**Issue:**
- Dev has **explicit provider configuration** with GKE cluster data sources (lines 25-37 in dev/main.tf)
- QA/Prod have **empty provider blocks** `provider "kubernetes" {}` (line 18 in both)

**Files Affected:**
- `/home/nathan_booth/code/konecta-ix-services/kix-services-crewai/environments/dev/terraform/main.tf` (lines 25-37)
- `/home/nathan_booth/code/konecta-ix-services/kix-services-crewai/environments/qa/terraform/main.tf` (line 18)
- `/home/nathan_booth/code/konecta-ix-services/kix-services-crewai/environments/prod/terraform/main.tf` (line 18)

**Impact:**
- QA/Prod rely on external configuration (KUBECONFIG environment variable)
- Different authentication mechanisms between environments
- Potential CI/CD pipeline failures if environment variables not set correctly
- Less explicit dependency tracking

**Recommendation:**
Standardize on dev's explicit configuration pattern across all environments for better reproducibility and clearer dependency management.

```hcl
# Apply to QA and Prod
data "google_client_config" "default" {}

data "google_container_cluster" "gke" {
  project  = var.gke_project_id
  name     = var.gke_cluster_name
  location = var.region
}

provider "kubernetes" {
  host                   = "https://${data.google_container_cluster.gke.endpoint}"
  token                  = data.google_client_config.default.access_token
  cluster_ca_certificate = base64decode(data.google_container_cluster.gke.master_auth[0].cluster_ca_certificate)
}
```

---

### 3. **Secret Manager Project ID Inconsistency**

**Severity:** HIGH  
**Risk:** Security boundary violations, access control issues

**Issue:**
- Dev uses separate `security_project_id` variable for Secret Manager (kd-ix-eur-dev-security)
- QA/Prod use `gke_project_id` for Secret Manager (kd-ix-eur-qa-gke, kd-ix-eur-prod-gke)

**Files Affected:**
- `/home/nathan_booth/code/konecta-ix-services/kix-services-crewai/environments/dev/terraform/main.tf` (line 148)
- `/home/nathan_booth/code/konecta-ix-services/kix-services-crewai/environments/qa/terraform/main.tf` (line 105)
- `/home/nathan_booth/code/konecta-ix-services/kix-services-crewai/environments/prod/terraform/main.tf` (line 105)

**Impact:**
- Inconsistent security boundary enforcement
- Dev secrets isolated in security project, QA/Prod secrets in GKE project
- Different IAM permissions required per environment
- Violates principle of least privilege if QA/Prod should use dedicated security projects

**Recommendation:**
Decide on the target pattern:
- **Option A (Recommended):** All environments use dedicated security project (align QA/Prod with dev)
- **Option B:** All environments use GKE project (align dev with QA/Prod)

Based on enterprise best practices, **Option A is strongly recommended** - secrets should live in dedicated security projects.

---

### 4. **Missing Variables in QA/Prod**

**Severity:** HIGH  
**Risk:** Plan failures, missing dependencies

**Issue:**
- Dev defines `gke_cluster_name` variable (needed for kubernetes provider)
- QA/Prod do NOT define this variable, yet would need it if using explicit K8s provider config

**Files Affected:**
- `/home/nathan_booth/code/konecta-ix-services/kix-services-crewai/environments/dev/terraform/variables.tf` (lines 25-29)
- `/home/nathan_booth/code/konecta-ix-services/kix-services-crewai/environments/qa/terraform/variables.tf` (missing)
- `/home/nathan_booth/code/konecta-ix-services/kix-services-crewai/environments/prod/terraform/variables.tf` (missing)

**Recommendation:**
Add `gke_cluster_name` variable to QA and Prod:

```hcl
variable "gke_cluster_name" {
  description = "GKE cluster name"
  type        = string
  default     = "kd-ix-eur-{env}-cluster"  # Replace {env}
}
```

### 13. **Local Variable Optimization Opportunity**

**Severity:** LOW  
**Impact:** DRY improvement

**Issue:**
Dev has a smart pattern for service account email (line 13) to avoid unknown value issues:
```hcl
crewai_sa_email = "crewai-platform-${var.environment}@${var.gke_project_id}.iam.gserviceaccount.com"
```

QA/Prod directly reference `google_service_account.crewai_platform.email` in accessor_service_accounts, which can cause for_each issues.

---

### 14. **Random Password Keeper Configuration Missing**

**Severity:** LOW  
**Risk:** Password regeneration on Terraform state issues

**Issue:**
All `random_password` resources lack `keeper` blocks for lifecycle management.

**Files Affected:**
- All environments' main.tf files (lines 73-86 in dev, 46-59 in qa/prod)

**Recommendation:**
Add keeper blocks to prevent accidental password rotation:

```hcl
resource "random_password" "db_password" {
  length  = 32
  special = false
  
  keepers = {
    # Password rotates only when database username changes
    database_user = "crewai"
  }
}
```

---

### 15. **Missing Variable Validation**

**Severity:** LOW  
**Impact:** Better error messages

**Issue:**
Variables lack validation rules, particularly for:
- `environment` (should be one of: dev, qa, prod)
- `region` (should be valid GCP region)
- OAuth variables (should not be empty in production)

**Recommendation:**
Add validation blocks:

```hcl
variable "environment" {
  description = "Environment name"
  type        = string
  default     = "dev"
  
  validation {
    condition     = contains(["dev", "qa", "prod"], var.environment)
    error_message = "Environment must be one of: dev, qa, prod."
  }
}

variable "oauth_client_id" {
  description = "WorkOS OAuth client ID"
  type        = string
  default     = ""
  sensitive   = true
  
  validation {
    condition     = var.environment != "prod" || var.oauth_client_id != ""
    error_message = "OAuth client ID cannot be empty in production environment."
  }
}
```

---

### 16. **Resource Tagging Inconsistency**

**Severity:** LOW  
**Impact:** Cost tracking and resource management

**Issue:**
Some resources use `local.labels`, others don't apply labels at all.

**Examples:**
- GCS modules: use labels via module (good)
- Service accounts: NO labels applied
- SQL resources: NO labels applied
- IAM bindings: NO labels supported (expected)

**Recommendation:**
Apply labels consistently to all labelable resources:

```hcl
resource "google_service_account" "crewai_platform" {
  project      = var.gke_project_id
  account_id   = "crewai-platform-${var.environment}"
  display_name = "CrewAI Platform - ${var.environment}"
  description  = "Service account for CrewAI platform workloads"
  
  # Add labels for better resource management
  labels = {
    app         = "crewai"
    environment = var.environment
    managed-by  = "terraform"
  }
}
```

Note: Check GCP resource support for labels before adding.

---

### 17. **Missing Resource Dependencies**

**Severity:** LOW  
**Risk:** Race conditions during apply

**Issue:**
Some resources lack explicit `depends_on` that could prevent race conditions:
- `kubernetes_secret.crewai_credentials` depends on namespace creation but only in QA/Prod (via `depends_on = [module.gke_config]`)
- Dev's inline namespace resources don't have explicit depends_on for secrets

**Recommendation:**
Add explicit dependencies where resource creation order matters:

```hcl
resource "kubernetes_secret" "crewai_credentials" {
  metadata {
    name      = "crewai-credentials"
    namespace = kubernetes_namespace.crewai_platform.metadata[0].name
    labels    = local.labels
  }
  
  # ... data block ...
  
  depends_on = [kubernetes_namespace.crewai_platform]
}
```

---

### 18. **No Lifecycle Rules on State-Managed Resources**

**Severity:** LOW  
**Risk:** Accidental resource destruction

**Issue:**
Critical resources like databases and service accounts lack lifecycle protection.

**Recommendation:**
Add lifecycle blocks for production-critical resources:

```hcl
resource "google_sql_database" "production" {
  provider = google
  project  = var.platform_data_project
  instance = data.google_sql_database_instance.platform.name
  name     = "crewai_plus_production"
  
  lifecycle {
    prevent_destroy = true  # Especially for prod environment
  }
}

resource "google_service_account" "crewai_platform" {
  project      = var.gke_project_id
  account_id   = "crewai-platform-${var.environment}"
  display_name = "CrewAI Platform - ${var.environment}"
  
  lifecycle {
    prevent_destroy = true  # Prevent accidental deletion
  }
}
```

---

## DRY Improvement Opportunities

### 19. **Environment-Specific Tfvars Files**

**Severity:** LOW  
**Impact:** Reduce hardcoded defaults

**Current State:**
All environment-specific values are hardcoded as variable defaults.

**Recommendation:**
Create environment-specific `.tfvars` files:

```hcl
# environments/dev/terraform/terraform.tfvars
gke_project_id          = "kd-ix-eur-dev-gke"
platform_data_project   = "kd-ix-eur-dev-platform-data"
security_project_id     = "kd-ix-eur-dev-security"
region                  = "europe-west1"
environment             = "dev"
cloud_sql_instance_name = "kd-ix-eur-dev-cloudsql-platform"
gke_cluster_name        = "kd-ix-eur-dev-cluster"
```

Remove defaults from variables.tf, making them required inputs. This makes the configuration more explicit and easier to validate.

---

### 20. **Shared Locals File Opportunity**

**Severity:** LOW  
**Impact:** Code reuse

**Pattern Identified:**
All environments define identical `labels` local block.

**Recommendation:**
Consider creating a shared locals.tf or referencing a common module for standard label definitions.

---

### 21. **Module Version Pinning**

**Severity:** MEDIUM  
**Risk:** Unexpected changes from module updates

**Issue:**
All remote modules use `?ref=main` which tracks the latest main branch.

**Files Affected:**
All module source declarations across all environments.

**Recommendation:**
Pin to specific Git tags or commit SHAs:

```hcl
module "gcs_data" {
  source = "github.com/konecta-ix-platform/kix-platform-modules//terraform/gcp/data-services/gcs?ref=v1.2.3"
  # ... rest of config
}
```

This provides:
- Predictable deployments
- Easier rollback
- Clear change tracking
- Safer production deployments

## Testing Recommendations

Before applying any changes:

1. **Validation:**
```bash
cd environments/{env}/terraform
terraform fmt -check
terraform init -backend=false
terraform validate
```

2. **Plan Review:**
```bash
terraform plan -out=tfplan
terraform show -json tfplan | jq '.resource_changes[] | select(.change.actions != ["no-op"])'
```

3. **State Inspection:**

## Repository Review Summary

### Critical Issues Requiring Immediate Attention

| Issue | Source | Impact |
|-------|--------|--------|
| **ArgoCD references feature branch** | application.yaml:33 | Sync will break when branch is deleted |
| **Environment divergence** | Terraform | Dev uses inline K8s resources, QA/Prod use gke-config module |
| **Missing oci:// prefix** | application.yaml:22 | May fail OCI chart pulling |
| **Chart name mismatch** | CLAUDE.md vs application.yaml | `crewai-enterprise` vs `crewai-platform` |
| **Cloud Build trigger** | image-mirror.tf | References `develop` branch that doesn't exist |

### High Priority Issues

1. **Kubernetes provider inconsistency** - Dev has explicit GKE data lookup, QA/Prod have empty `provider "kubernetes" {}`
2. **Security project ID inconsistency** - Dev uses dedicated security project, QA/Prod store secrets in GKE project
3. **Missing variables in QA/Prod** - `gke_cluster_name`, `security_project_id` not defined
4. **Workload Identity binding mismatch** - Dev uses `crewai-dev-sa`, QA/Prod use `crewai-platform-sa`
5. **Dev missing pod security contexts** - All security contexts and probes commented out
6. **Module version pinning** - All modules use `?ref=main` instead of pinned tags

### Medium Priority Issues

1. Backend bucket naming inconsistency (dev: `kd-tfstate-services-dev`, QA/Prod: `kd-tfstate-{env}`)
2. Image mirroring only in dev environment
3. Network policies conditionally enabled but not explicitly set
4. Hardcoded cluster IP in dev ArgoCD application
5. Missing helm registry secret in dev
6. GitHub impersonation IAM missing in QA/Prod

### Documentation Gaps

1. **SETUP.md outdated** - Phase 1 & 2 don't match current directory structure
2. **Missing environment variables reference** guide
3. **Sparse inline code comments** in Terraform files
4. **Missing upgrade/rollback runbook**

### Quick Wins

1. Update ArgoCD `targetRevision` to `main` (or create `develop` branch)
2. Fix Cloud Build trigger branch reference
3. Clean up stray `#` characters in values.yaml (lines 57, 67, 77, 86, 96, etc.)
4. Pin container image versions in image-mirror.yaml
5. Add `oci://` prefix to repoURL if needed

```
     1→# CrewAI Platform - Dev Environment
     2→# Composes remote modules from kix-platform-modules + inline resources
     3→
     4→locals {
     5→  labels = {
     6→    app         = "crewai"
     7→    environment = var.environment
     8→    managed-by  = "terraform"
     9→    platform    = "crewai"
    10→  }
    11→
    12→  # Construct SA email statically to avoid for_each unknown value issues
    13→  crewai_sa_email = "crewai-platform-${var.environment}@${var.gke_project_id}.iam.gserviceaccount.com"
    14→}
    15→
    16→# ---------------------------------------------------------------------------
    17→# Providers
    18→# ---------------------------------------------------------------------------
    19→
    20→provider "google" {
    21→  project = var.gke_project_id
    22→  region  = var.region
    23→}
    24→
    25→data "google_client_config" "default" {}
    26→
    27→data "google_container_cluster" "gke" {
    28→  project  = var.gke_project_id
    29→  name     = var.gke_cluster_name
    30→  location = var.region
    31→}
    32→
    33→provider "kubernetes" {
    34→  host                   = "https://${data.google_container_cluster.gke.endpoint}"
    35→  token                  = data.google_client_config.default.access_token
    36→  cluster_ca_certificate = base64decode(data.google_container_cluster.gke.master_auth[0].cluster_ca_certificate)
    37→}
    38→
    39→# ---------------------------------------------------------------------------
    40→# Data: Existing Cloud SQL Instance
    41→# ---------------------------------------------------------------------------
    42→
    43→data "google_sql_database_instance" "platform" {
    44→  project = var.platform_data_project
    45→  name    = var.cloud_sql_instance_name
    46→}
    47→
    48→# ---------------------------------------------------------------------------
    49→# Cloud SQL: Databases + User on Existing Instance
    50→# ---------------------------------------------------------------------------
    51→
    52→resource "google_sql_database" "production" {
    53→  provider = google
    54→  project  = var.platform_data_project
    55→  instance = data.google_sql_database_instance.platform.name
    56→  name     = "crewai_plus_production"
    57→}
    58→
    59→resource "google_sql_database" "cable" {
    60→  provider = google
    61→  project  = var.platform_data_project
    62→  instance = data.google_sql_database_instance.platform.name
    63→  name     = "crewai_plus_cable_production"
    64→}
    65→
    66→resource "google_sql_database" "oauth" {
    67→  provider = google
    68→  project  = var.platform_data_project
    69→  instance = data.google_sql_database_instance.platform.name
    70→  name     = "crewai_plus_oauth_db"
    71→}
    72→
    73→resource "random_password" "db_password" {
    74→  length  = 32
    75→  special = false
    76→}
    77→
    78→resource "random_password" "secret_key_base" {
    79→  length  = 128
    80→  special = true
    81→}
    82→
    83→resource "random_password" "platform_master_key" {
    84→  length  = 32
    85→  special = false
    86→}
    87→
    88→resource "google_sql_user" "crewai" {
    89→  provider = google
    90→  project  = var.platform_data_project
    91→  instance = data.google_sql_database_instance.platform.name
    92→  name     = "crewai"
    93→  password = random_password.db_password.result
    94→}
    95→
    96→# ---------------------------------------------------------------------------
    97→# GCS: Data + Logs Buckets (remote module)
    98→# ---------------------------------------------------------------------------
    99→
   100→module "gcs_data" {
   101→  source = "github.com/konecta-ix-platform/kix-platform-modules//terraform/gcp/data-services/gcs?ref=main"
   102→
   103→  project_id  = var.gke_project_id
   104→  environment = var.environment
   105→  use_case    = "crewai"
   106→  name_suffix = "data"
   107→  cost_center = "platform"
   108→  location    = var.region
   109→}
   110→
   111→module "gcs_logs" {
   112→  source = "github.com/konecta-ix-platform/kix-platform-modules//terraform/gcp/data-services/gcs?ref=main"
   113→
   114→  project_id  = var.gke_project_id
   115→  environment = var.environment
   116→  use_case    = "crewai"
   117→  name_suffix = "logs"
   118→  cost_center = "platform"
   119→  location    = var.region
   120→}
   121→
   122→# ---------------------------------------------------------------------------
   123→# GCP Service Account (inline to avoid circular deps with HMAC/secrets)
   124→# ---------------------------------------------------------------------------
   125→
   126→resource "google_service_account" "crewai_platform" {
   127→  project      = var.gke_project_id
   128→  account_id   = "crewai-platform-${var.environment}"
   129→  display_name = "CrewAI Platform - ${var.environment}"
   130→}
   131→
   132→# ---------------------------------------------------------------------------

   134→# ---------------------------------------------------------------------------
   135→
   136→resource "google_storage_hmac_key" "crewai" {
   137→  project               = var.gke_project_id
   138→  service_account_email = google_service_account.crewai_platform.email
   139→}
   140→
   141→# ---------------------------------------------------------------------------
   142→# Secret Manager: Application Secrets (one module call per credential)
   143→# ---------------------------------------------------------------------------
   144→
   145→module "secret_db_credentials" {
   146→  source = "github.com/konecta-ix-platform/kix-platform-modules//terraform/gcp/secret-manager?ref=main"
   147→
   148→  project_id  = var.security_project_id
   149→  environment = var.environment
   150→  secret_id   = "crewai-db-credentials-${var.environment}"
   151→  secret_data = jsonencode({
   152→    password = random_password.db_password.result
   153→    host     = data.google_sql_database_instance.platform.private_ip_address
   154→    port     = 5432
   155→    database = "crewai_plus_production"
   156→    username = "crewai"
   157→  })
   158→
   159→  accessor_service_accounts = [local.crewai_sa_email]
   160→  additional_labels         = local.labels
   161→}
   162→
   163→module "secret_gcs_credentials" {
   164→  source = "github.com/konecta-ix-platform/kix-platform-modules//terraform/gcp/secret-manager?ref=main"
   165→
   166→  project_id  = var.security_project_id
   167→  environment = var.environment
   168→  secret_id   = "crewai-gcs-credentials-${var.environment}"
   169→  secret_data = jsonencode({
   170→    access_key_id     = google_storage_hmac_key.crewai.access_id
   171→    secret_access_key = google_storage_hmac_key.crewai.secret
   172→  })
   173→
   174→  accessor_service_accounts = [local.crewai_sa_email]

   176→}
   177→
   178→module "secret_oauth_credentials" {
   179→  source = "github.com/konecta-ix-platform/kix-platform-modules//terraform/gcp/secret-manager?ref=main"
   180→
   181→  project_id  = var.security_project_id
   182→  environment = var.environment
   183→  secret_id   = "crewai-oauth-credentials-${var.environment}"
   184→  secret_data = jsonencode({

   187→    api_key   = var.oauth_api_key
   188→    domain    = var.oauth_domain
   189→  })
   190→
   191→  accessor_service_accounts = [local.crewai_sa_email]
   192→  additional_labels         = local.labels
   193→}
   194→
   195→# ---------------------------------------------------------------------------
   196→# IAM Bindings for Service Account
   197→# ---------------------------------------------------------------------------
   198→
   199→resource "google_project_iam_member" "cloudsql_client" {
   200→  project = var.platform_data_project
   201→  role    = "roles/cloudsql.client"
   202→  member  = "serviceAccount:${google_service_account.crewai_platform.email}"
   203→}
   204→
   205→resource "google_storage_bucket_iam_member" "data_bucket" {
   206→  bucket = module.gcs_data.bucket_name
   207→  role   = "roles/storage.objectAdmin"
   208→  member = "serviceAccount:${google_service_account.crewai_platform.email}"
   209→}
   210→
   211→resource "google_storage_bucket_iam_member" "logs_bucket" {
   212→  bucket = module.gcs_logs.bucket_name

   215→}
   216→
   217→resource "google_service_account_iam_member" "workload_identity" {
   218→  service_account_id = google_service_account.crewai_platform.name
   219→  role               = "roles/iam.workloadIdentityUser"

   222→

   228→

   251→# ---------------------------------------------------------------------------

   288→
   289→resource "kubernetes_service_account" "crewai_crews" {

   302→resource "kubernetes_limit_range" "crewai_platform" {

   308→  spec {

   348→resource "kubernetes_resource_quota" "crewai_platform" {

   418→# Grant AR reader role to the platform SA (for pulling images via Workload Identity)
   419→resource "google_artifact_registry_repository_iam_member" "platform_reader" {
   420→  project    = var.gke_project_id
   421→  location   = var.region
   422→  repository = google_artifact_registry_repository.crewai_builder.name
   423→  role       = "roles/artifactregistry.reader"
   424→  member     = "serviceAccount:${google_service_account.crewai_platform.email}"
   425→}
   426→
   427→# ---------------------------------------------------------------------------
   428→# K8s ConfigMap: Database Configuration (required by CrewAI chart)
   429→# ---------------------------------------------------------------------------
   430→
   431→resource "kubernetes_config_map" "database_config" {
   432→  metadata {
   433→    name      = "crewai-${var.environment}-database-config"
   434→    namespace = kubernetes_namespace.crewai_platform.metadata[0].name
   435→    labels    = local.labels
   436→  }
   437→
   438→  data = {
   439→    "database.yml" = yamlencode({
   440→      production = {
   441→        adapter  = "postgresql"
   442→        host     = "cloud-sql-proxy"
   443→        port     = 5432
   444→        database = "crewai_plus_production"
   445→        username = "crewai"
   446→        pool     = 5
   447→      }
   448→      cable = {
   449→        adapter  = "postgresql"
   450→        host     = "cloud-sql-proxy"
   451→        port     = 5432
   452→        database = "crewai_plus_cable_production"
   453→        username = "crewai"
   454→        pool     = 5
   455→      }
   456→    })
   457→  }
   458→}
   459→
   460→# ---------------------------------------------------------------------------
   461→# Cloud SQL Proxy: Standalone Deployment for shared DB access
   462→# ---------------------------------------------------------------------------
   463→
   464→resource "kubernetes_deployment" "cloud_sql_proxy" {
   465→  metadata {
   466→    name      = "cloud-sql-proxy"
   467→    namespace = kubernetes_namespace.crewai_platform.metadata[0].name
   468→    labels    = merge(local.labels, { component = "cloud-sql-proxy" })
   469→  }
   470→
   471→  spec {
   472→    replicas = 1
   473→
   474→    selector {
   475→      match_labels = {
   476→        app       = "cloud-sql-proxy"
   477→        component = "cloud-sql-proxy"
   478→      }
   479→    }
   480→
   481→    template {
   482→      metadata {
   483→        labels = {
   484→          app       = "cloud-sql-proxy"
   485→          component = "cloud-sql-proxy"
   486→        }
   487→      }
   488→
   489→      spec {
   490→        service_account_name = kubernetes_service_account.crewai_platform.metadata[0].name
   491→
   492→        container {
   493→          name  = "cloud-sql-proxy"
   494→          image = "gcr.io/cloud-sql-connectors/cloud-sql-proxy:2.8.2"
   495→
   496→          args = [
   497→            "--structured-logs",
   498→            "--private-ip",
   499→            "--address=0.0.0.0",
   500→            "--port=5432",
   501→            "--health-check",
   502→            "--http-port=9090",
   503→            "--http-address=0.0.0.0",
   504→            "${var.platform_data_project}:${var.region}:${var.cloud_sql_instance_name}"
   505→          ]
   506→
   507→          port {
   508→            container_port = 5432
   509→            name           = "postgres"
   510→          }
   511→
   512→          port {
   513→            container_port = 9090
   514→            name           = "http"
   515→          }
   516→
   517→          resources {
   518→            requests = {
   519→              cpu    = "100m"
   520→              memory = "128Mi"
   521→            }
   522→            limits = {
   523→              cpu    = "500m"
   524→              memory = "512Mi"
   525→            }
   526→          }
   527→
   528→          security_context {
   529→            run_as_non_root            = true
   530→            run_as_user                = 65532
   531→            allow_privilege_escalation = false
   532→          }
   533→
   534→          liveness_probe {
   535→            http_get {
   536→              path = "/liveness"
   537→              port = 9090
   538→            }
   539→            initial_delay_seconds = 30
   540→            period_seconds        = 10
   541→          }
   542→
   543→          readiness_probe {
   544→            http_get {
   545→              path = "/readiness"
   546→              port = 9090
   547→            }
   548→            initial_delay_seconds = 10
   549→            period_seconds        = 5
   550→          }
   551→        }
   552→      }
   553→    }
   554→  }
   555→}
   556→
   557→resource "kubernetes_service" "cloud_sql_proxy" {
   558→  metadata {
   559→    name      = "cloud-sql-proxy"
   560→    namespace = kubernetes_namespace.crewai_platform.metadata[0].name
   561→    labels    = merge(local.labels, { component = "cloud-sql-proxy" })
   562→  }
   563→
   564→  spec {
   565→    selector = {
   566→      app       = "cloud-sql-proxy"
   567→      component = "cloud-sql-proxy"
   568→    }
   569→
   570→    port {
   571→      port        = 5432
   572→      target_port = 5432
   573→      name        = "postgres"
   574→    }
   575→
   576→    type = "ClusterIP"
   577→  }
   578→}
```

```json
{
  "file_path": "/home/nathan_booth/code/konecta-ix-services/kix-services-crewai/environments/dev/terraform/service-accounts.tf",
  "content": "# ---------------------------------------------------------------------------\n# Service Accounts and IAM Bindings\n#\n# GCP service account for CrewAI platform workloads with Workload Identity.\n# Includes all IAM bindings for Cloud SQL, GCS, and Artifact Registry access.\n# ---------------------------------------------------------------------------\n\n# ---------------------------------------------------------------------------\n# GCP Service Account\n# ---------------------------------------------------------------------------\n# Created inline (not via module) to avoid circular dependencies with HMAC keys\n# and secrets that reference the service account email.\n\nresource \"google_service_account\" \"crewai_platform\" {\n  project      = var.gke_project_id\n  account_id   = \"crewai-platform-${var.environment}\"\n  display_name = \"CrewAI Platform - ${var.environment}\"\n  description  = \"Service account for CrewAI platform workloads with Workload Identity\"\n}\n\n# ---------------------------------------------------------------------------\n# IAM: Cloud SQL Access\n# ---------------------------------------------------------------------------\n# Allows the service account to connect to Cloud SQL via the Cloud SQL Proxy.\n\nresource \"google_project_iam_member\" \"cloudsql_client\" {\n  project = var.platform_data_project\n  role    = \"roles/cloudsql.client\"\n  member  = \"serviceAccount:${google_service_account.crewai_platform.email}\"\n}\n\n# ---------------------------------------------------------------------------\n# IAM: GCS Bucket Access\n# ---------------------------------------------------------------------------\n# Data bucket: full object admin for reading and writing application data.\n# Logs bucket: object creator only for write-only audit logging.\n\nresource \"google_storage_bucket_iam_member\" \"data_bucket\" {\n  bucket = module.gcs_data.bucket_name\n  role   = \"roles/storage.objectAdmin\"\n  member = \"serviceAccount:${google_service_account.crewai_platform.email}\"\n}\n\nresource \"google_storage_bucket_iam_member\" \"logs_bucket\" {\n  bucket = module.gcs_logs.bucket_name\n  role   = \"roles/storage.objectCreator\"\n  member = \"serviceAccount:${google_service_account.crewai_platform.email}\"\n}\n\n# ---------------------------------------------------------------------------\n# IAM: Workload Identity Binding\n# ---------------------------------------------------------------------------\n# Allows the Kubernetes service account to impersonate the GCP service account.\n# This enables pods to authenticate to GCP services without service account keys.\n\nresource \"google_service_account_iam_member\" \"workload_identity\" {\n  service_account_id = google_service_account.crewai_platform.name\n  role               = \"roles/iam.workloadIdentityUser\"\n  member             = \"serviceAccount:${var.gke_project_id}.svc.id.goog[crewai-${var.environment}/crewai-${var.environment}-sa]\"\n}\n\n# ---------------------------------------------------------------------------\n# IAM: GitHub Actions Impersonation\n# ---------------------------------------------------------------------------\n# Allows GitHub Actions to impersonate this service account for CI/CD.\n# The GitHub service account is created separately in the platform infrastructure.\n\nresource \"google_service_account_iam_member\" \"github_impersonation\" {\n  service_account_id = google_service_account.crewai_platform.name\n  role               = \"roles/iam.serviceAccountTokenCreator\"\n  member             = \"serviceAccount:github-kix-services-crewai-${var.environment}@${var.gke_project_id}.iam.gserviceaccount.com\"\n}\n"
}
```

```json
{
  "file_path": "/home/nathan_booth/code/konecta-ix-services/kix-services-crewai/environments/dev/terraform/kubernetes.tf",
  "content": "# ---------------------------------------------------------------------------\n# Kubernetes Resources\n#\n# Namespaces, service accounts, limit ranges, and resource quotas for the\n# CrewAI platform. Created inline to avoid gke-config module's defaults.\n# ---------------------------------------------------------------------------\n\n# ---------------------------------------------------------------------------\n# Namespaces\n# ---------------------------------------------------------------------------\n# Two namespaces separate platform services from crew workloads for isolation.\n# - crewai-{env}: Platform services (web, worker, buildkit)\n# - crewai-crews-{env}: Crew execution workloads (dynamically created pods)\n\nresource \"kubernetes_namespace\" \"crewai_platform\" {\n  metadata {\n    name = \"crewai-${var.environment}\"\n    labels = merge(local.labels, {\n      \"app.kubernetes.io/name\" = \"crewai-platform\"\n    })\n  }\n}\n\nresource \"kubernetes_namespace\" \"crewai_crews\" {\n  metadata {\n    name = \"crewai-crews-${var.environment}\"\n    labels = merge(local.labels, {\n      \"app.kubernetes.io/name\" = \"crewai-crews\"\n    })\n  }\n}\n\n# ---------------------------------------------------------------------------\n# Kubernetes Service Accounts\n# ---------------------------------------------------------------------------\n# Platform SA has Workload Identity annotation to authenticate to GCP.\n# Crews SA is simpler - crew pods don't need direct GCP access.\n# Token automount is disabled for security; pods explicitly mount if needed.\n\nresource \"kubernetes_service_account\" \"crewai_platform\" {\n  metadata {\n    name      = \"crewai-${var.environment}-sa\"\n    namespace = kubernetes_namespace.crewai_platform.metadata[0].name\n    labels    = local.labels\n    annotations = {\n      \"iam.gke.io/gcp-service-account\" = google_service_account.crewai_platform.email\n    }\n  }\n  automount_service_account_token = false\n}\n\nresource \"kubernetes_service_account\" \"crewai_crews\" {\n  metadata {\n    name      = \"crewai-crews-sa\"\n    namespace = kubernetes_namespace.crewai_crews.metadata[0].name\n    labels    = local.labels\n  }\n  automount_service_account_token = false\n}\n\n# ---------------------------------------------------------------------------\n# Limit Ranges\n# ---------------------------------------------------------------------------\n# Default resource limits for containers without explicit limits set.\n# Platform namespace has lower defaults; crews namespace allows more resources.\n\nresource \"kubernetes_limit_range\" \"crewai_platform\" {\n  metadata {\n    name      = \"crewai-platform-limits\"\n    namespace = kubernetes_namespace.crewai_platform.metadata[0].name\n    labels    = local.labels\n  }\n  spec {\n    limit {\n      type = \"Container\"\n      default = {\n        cpu    = \"500m\"\n        memory = \"512Mi\"\n      }\n      default_request = {\n        cpu    = \"100m\"\n        memory = \"128Mi\"\n      }\n    }\n  }\n}\n\nresource \"kubernetes_limit_range\" \"crewai_crews\" {\n  metadata {\n    name      = \"crewai-crews-limits\"\n    namespace = kubernetes_namespace.crewai_crews.metadata[0].name\n    labels    = local.labels\n  }\n  spec {\n    limit {\n      type = \"Container\"\n      default = {\n        cpu    = \"1\"\n        memory = \"1Gi\"\n      }\n      default_request = {\n        cpu    = \"250m\"\n        memory = \"256Mi\"\n      }\n    }\n  }\n}\n\n# ---------------------------------------------------------------------------\n# Resource Quotas\n# ---------------------------------------------------------------------------\n# Hard limits on total resources consumed per namespace.\n# Platform namespace is smaller; crews namespace allows burst capacity.\n\nresource \"kubernetes_resource_quota\" \"crewai_platform\" {\n  metadata {\n    name      = \"crewai-platform-quota\"\n    namespace = kubernetes_namespace.crewai_platform.metadata[0].name\n    labels    = local.labels\n  }\n  spec {\n    hard = {\n      \"requests.cpu\"         = \"20\"\n      \"requests.memory\"      = \"40Gi\"\n      \"limits.cpu\"           = \"40\"\n      \"limits.memory\"        = \"80Gi\"\n      pods                   = \"50\"\n      services               = \"20\"\n      secrets                = \"30\"\n      configmaps             = \"30\"\n      persistentvolumeclaims = \"10\"\n    }\n  }\n  depends_on = [kubernetes_limit_range.crewai_platform]\n}\n\nresource \"kubernetes_resource_quota\" \"crewai_crews\" {\n  metadata {\n    name      = \"crewai-crews-quota\"\n    namespace = kubernetes_namespace.crewai_crews.metadata[0].name\n    labels    = local.labels\n  }\n  spec {\n    hard = {\n      \"requests.cpu\"         = \"40\"\n      \"requests.memory\"      = \"80Gi\"\n      \"limits.cpu\"           = \"80\"\n      \"limits.memory\"        = \"160Gi\"\n      pods                   = \"100\"\n      services               = \"10\"\n      secrets                = \"20\"\n      configmaps             = \"20\"\n      persistentvolumeclaims = \"10\"\n    }\n  }\n  depends_on = [kubernetes_limit_range.crewai_crews]\n}\n"
}
```

```json
{
  "file_path": "/home/nathan_booth/code/konecta-ix-services/kix-services-crewai/environments/dev/terraform/secrets.tf",
  "content": "# ---------------------------------------------------------------------------\n# Secrets Management\n#\n# GCP Secret Manager for audit/backup and Kubernetes secrets for pod consumption.\n# Uses the single-secret-per-call pattern for the secret-manager module.\n# ---------------------------------------------------------------------------\n\n# ---------------------------------------------------------------------------\n# Random Passwords\n# ---------------------------------------------------------------------------\n# Generated once and stored in state. To rotate, taint and re-apply.\n\nresource \"random_password\" \"db_password\" {\n  length  = 32\n  special = false\n}\n\nresource \"random_password\" \"secret_key_base\" {\n  length  = 128\n  special = true\n}\n\nresource \"random_password\" \"platform_master_key\" {\n  length  = 32\n  special = false\n}\n\n# ---------------------------------------------------------------------------\n# Secret Manager: Database Credentials\n# ---------------------------------------------------------------------------\n# Stored in the security project for centralized secret management.\n# Pods use the K8s secret below; this is for audit and disaster recovery.\n\nmodule \"secret_db_credentials\" {\n  source = \"github.com/konecta-ix-platform/kix-platform-modules//terraform/gcp/secret-manager?ref=main\"\n\n  project_id  = var.security_project_id\n  environment = var.environment\n  secret_id   = \"crewai-db-credentials-${var.environment}\"\n  secret_data = jsonencode({\n    password = random_password.db_password.result\n    host     = data.google_sql_database_instance.platform.private_ip_address\n    port     = 5432\n    database = \"crewai_plus_production\"\n    username = \"crewai\"\n  })\n\n  accessor_service_accounts = [local.crewai_sa_email]\n  additional_labels         = local.labels\n}\n\n# ---------------------------------------------------------------------------\n# Secret Manager: GCS Credentials (HMAC Keys)\n# ---------------------------------------------------------------------------\n# HMAC keys for S3-compatible GCS access (used by Rails ActiveStorage).\n\nmodule \"secret_gcs_credentials\" {\n  source = \"github.com/konecta-ix-platform/kix-platform-modules//terraform/gcp/secret-manager?ref=main\"\n\n  project_id  = var.security_project_id\n  environment = var.environment\n  secret_id   = \"crewai-gcs-credentials-${var.environment}\"\n  secret_data = jsonencode({\n    access_key_id     = google_storage_hmac_key.crewai.access_id\n    secret_access_key = google_storage_hmac_key.crewai.secret\n  })\n\n  accessor_service_accounts = [local.crewai_sa_email]\n  additional_labels         = local.labels\n}\n\n# ---------------------------------------------------------------------------\n# Secret Manager: OAuth Credentials (WorkOS)\n# ---------------------------------------------------------------------------\n\nmodule \"secret_oauth_credentials\" {\n  source = \"github.com/konecta-ix-platform/kix-platform-modules//terraform/gcp/secret-manager?ref=main\"\n\n  project_id  = var.security_project_id\n  environment = var.environment\n  secret_id   = \"crewai-oauth-credentials-${var.environment}\"\n  secret_data = jsonencode({\n    provider  = \"workos\"\n    client_id = var.oauth_client_id\n    api_key   = var.oauth_api_key\n    domain    = var.oauth_domain\n  })\n\n  accessor_service_accounts = [local.crewai_sa_email]\n  additional_labels         = local.labels\n}\n\n# ---------------------------------------------------------------------------\n# Kubernetes Secret: Application Credentials\n# ---------------------------------------------------------------------------\n# Consumed by pods via envFrom secretRef in Helm values.\n# Maps Terraform-managed values to environment variable names expected by Rails.\n\nresource \"kubernetes_secret\" \"crewai_credentials\" {\n  metadata {\n    name      = \"crewai-credentials\"\n    namespace = kubernetes_namespace.crewai_platform.metadata[0].name\n    labels    = local.labels\n  }\n\n  data = {\n    DB_PASSWORD           = random_password.db_password.result\n    SECRET_KEY_BASE       = random_password.secret_key_base.result\n    PLATFORM_MASTER_KEY   = random_password.platform_master_key.result\n    AWS_ACCESS_KEY_ID     = google_storage_hmac_key.crewai.access_id\n    AWS_SECRET_ACCESS_KEY = google_storage_hmac_key.crewai.secret\n    WORKOS_CLIENT_ID      = var.oauth_client_id\n    WORKOS_API_KEY        = var.oauth_api_key\n  }\n}\n\n# ---------------------------------------------------------------------------\n# Kubernetes ConfigMap: Database Configuration\n# ---------------------------------------------------------------------------\n# Rails database.yml configuration for multi-database setup.\n\nresource \"kubernetes_config_map\" \"database_config\" {\n  metadata {\n    name      = \"crewai-${var.environment}-database-config\"\n    namespace = kubernetes_namespace.crewai_platform.metadata[0].name\n    labels    = local.labels\n  }\n\n  data = {\n    \"database.yml\" = yamlencode({\n      production = {\n        adapter  = \"postgresql\"\n        host     = \"cloud-sql-proxy\"\n        port     = 5432\n        database = \"crewai_plus_production\"\n        username = \"crewai\"\n        pool     = 5\n      }\n      cable = {\n        adapter  = \"postgresql\"\n        host     = \"cloud-sql-proxy\"\n        port     = 5432\n        database = \"crewai_plus_cable_production\"\n        username = \"crewai\"\n        pool     = 5\n      }\n    })\n  }\n}\n"
}
```

```json
{
  "file_path": "/home/nathan_booth/code/konecta-ix-services/kix-services-crewai/environments/dev/terraform/storage.tf",
  "content": "# ---------------------------------------------------------------------------\n# Cloud Storage\n#\n# GCS buckets for application data and logs using remote modules.\n# HMAC keys for S3-compatible access (used by Rails ActiveStorage).\n# ---------------------------------------------------------------------------\n\n# ---------------------------------------------------------------------------\n# GCS: Data Bucket\n# ---------------------------------------------------------------------------\n# Stores application data including file uploads and generated assets.\n\nmodule \"gcs_data\" {\n  source = \"github.com/konecta-ix-platform/kix-platform-modules//terraform/gcp/data-services/gcs?ref=main\"\n\n  project_id  = var.gke_project_id\n  environment = var.environment\n  use_case    = \"crewai\"\n  name_suffix = \"data\"\n  cost_center = \"platform\"\n  location    = var.region\n}\n\n# ---------------------------------------------------------------------------\n# GCS: Logs Bucket\n# ---------------------------------------------------------------------------\n# Stores application logs for audit and debugging purposes.\n\nmodule \"gcs_logs\" {\n  source = \"github.com/konecta-ix-platform/kix-platform-modules//terraform/gcp/data-services/gcs?ref=main\"\n\n  project_id  = var.gke_project_id\n  environment = var.environment\n  use_case    = \"crewai\"\n  name_suffix = \"logs\"\n  cost_center = \"platform\"\n  location    = var.region\n}\n\n# ---------------------------------------------------------------------------\n# HMAC Keys for S3-Compatible GCS Access\n# ---------------------------------------------------------------------------\n# CrewAI uses AWS SDK (S3-compatible) for storage. HMAC keys provide\n# access_key_id and secret_access_key equivalents for GCS.\n\nresource \"google_storage_hmac_key\" \"crewai\" {\n  project               = var.gke_project_id\n  service_account_email = google_service_account.crewai_platform.email\n}\n"
}
```

```json
{
  "file_path": "/home/nathan_booth/code/konecta-ix-services/kix-services-crewai/environments/dev/terraform/database.tf",
  "content": "# ---------------------------------------------------------------------------\n# Cloud SQL Database\n#\n# Databases and user on the shared platform Cloud SQL instance.\n# Cloud SQL Proxy deployment for secure database access from pods.\n# ---------------------------------------------------------------------------\n\n# ---------------------------------------------------------------------------\n# Data Source: Existing Cloud SQL Instance\n# ---------------------------------------------------------------------------\n# The Cloud SQL instance is managed at the platform level, not per-service.\n# This data source retrieves the instance details for database creation.\n\ndata \"google_sql_database_instance\" \"platform\" {\n  project = var.platform_data_project\n  name    = var.cloud_sql_instance_name\n}\n\n# ---------------------------------------------------------------------------\n# Cloud SQL: Databases\n# ---------------------------------------------------------------------------\n# CrewAI requires three PostgreSQL databases:\n# - production: Main application data\n# - cable: ActionCable (WebSocket) database for real-time features\n# - oauth: OAuth tokens and session data\n\nresource \"google_sql_database\" \"production\" {\n  provider = google\n  project  = var.platform_data_project\n  instance = data.google_sql_database_instance.platform.name\n  name     = \"crewai_plus_production\"\n}\n\nresource \"google_sql_database\" \"cable\" {\n  provider = google\n  project  = var.platform_data_project\n  instance = data.google_sql_database_instance.platform.name\n  name     = \"crewai_plus_cable_production\"\n}\n\nresource \"google_sql_database\" \"oauth\" {\n  provider = google\n  project  = var.platform_data_project\n  instance = data.google_sql_database_instance.platform.name\n  name     = \"crewai_plus_oauth_db\"\n}\n\n# ---------------------------------------------------------------------------\n# Cloud SQL: Database User\n# ---------------------------------------------------------------------------\n# Single user with access to all CrewAI databases.\n# Password is generated by random_password in secrets.tf.\n\nresource \"google_sql_user\" \"crewai\" {\n  provider = google\n  project  = var.platform_data_project\n  instance = data.google_sql_database_instance.platform.name\n  name     = \"crewai\"\n  password = random_password.db_password.result\n}\n\n# ---------------------------------------------------------------------------\n# Cloud SQL Proxy: Kubernetes Deployment\n# ---------------------------------------------------------------------------\n# Standalone proxy deployment provides shared database connectivity for all pods.\n# Uses Workload Identity to authenticate without service account keys.\n# All CrewAI pods connect to cloud-sql-proxy:5432 instead of the Cloud SQL IP.\n\nresource \"kubernetes_deployment\" \"cloud_sql_proxy\" {\n  metadata {\n    name      = \"cloud-sql-proxy\"\n    namespace = kubernetes_namespace.crewai_platform.metadata[0].name\n    labels    = merge(local.labels, { component = \"cloud-sql-proxy\" })\n  }\n\n  spec {\n    replicas = 1\n\n    selector {\n      match_labels = {\n        app       = \"cloud-sql-proxy\"\n        component = \"cloud-sql-proxy\"\n      }\n    }\n\n    template {\n      metadata {\n        labels = {\n          app       = \"cloud-sql-proxy\"\n          component = \"cloud-sql-proxy\"\n        }\n      }\n\n      spec {\n        service_account_name = kubernetes_service_account.crewai_platform.metadata[0].name\n\n        container {\n          name  = \"cloud-sql-proxy\"\n          image = \"gcr.io/cloud-sql-connectors/cloud-sql-proxy:2.8.2\"\n\n          args = [\n            \"--structured-logs\",\n            \"--private-ip\",\n            \"--address=0.0.0.0\",\n            \"--port=5432\",\n            \"--health-check\",\n            \"--http-port=9090\",\n            \"--http-address=0.0.0.0\",\n            \"${var.platform_data_project}:${var.region}:${var.cloud_sql_instance_name}\"\n          ]\n\n          port {\n            container_port = 5432\n            name           = \"postgres\"\n          }\n\n          port {\n            container_port = 9090\n            name           = \"http\"\n          }\n\n          resources {\n            requests = {\n              cpu    = \"100m\"\n              memory = \"128Mi\"\n            }\n            limits = {\n              cpu    = \"500m\"\n              memory = \"512Mi\"\n            }\n          }\n\n          # Security hardening: run as non-root with minimal capabilities\n          security_context {\n            run_as_non_root            = true\n            run_as_user                = 65532\n            allow_privilege_escalation = false\n          }\n\n          liveness_probe {\n            http_get {\n              path = \"/liveness\"\n              port = 9090\n            }\n            initial_delay_seconds = 30\n            period_seconds        = 10\n          }\n\n          readiness_probe {\n            http_get {\n              path = \"/readiness\"\n              port = 9090\n            }\n            initial_delay_seconds = 10\n            period_seconds        = 5\n          }\n        }\n      }\n    }\n  }\n}\n\n# ---------------------------------------------------------------------------\n# Cloud SQL Proxy: Kubernetes Service\n# ---------------------------------------------------------------------------\n# ClusterIP service exposes the proxy to other pods in the namespace.\n# Pods connect to cloud-sql-proxy:5432 as configured in database.yml.\n\nresource \"kubernetes_service\" \"cloud_sql_proxy\" {\n  metadata {\n    name      = \"cloud-sql-proxy\"\n    namespace = kubernetes_namespace.crewai_platform.metadata[0].name\n    labels    = merge(local.labels, { component = \"cloud-sql-proxy\" })\n  }\n\n  spec {\n    selector = {\n      app       = \"cloud-sql-proxy\"\n      component = \"cloud-sql-proxy\"\n    }\n\n    port {\n      port        = 5432\n      target_port = 5432\n      name        = \"postgres\"\n    }\n\n    type = \"ClusterIP\"\n  }\n}\n"
}
```

```json
{
  "file_path": "/home/nathan_booth/code/konecta-ix-services/kix-services-crewai/environments/dev/terraform/artifact-registry.tf",
  "content": "# ---------------------------------------------------------------------------\n# Artifact Registry\n#\n# Container registry for CrewAI crew image builds.\n# Platform SA has reader/writer access for building and pulling crew images.\n# ---------------------------------------------------------------------------\n\n# ---------------------------------------------------------------------------\n# Artifact Registry: Crew Builder Repository\n# ---------------------------------------------------------------------------\n# Stores container images built by CrewAI's BuildKit for crew workloads.\n# Each crew gets a unique image tag within this repository.\n\nresource \"google_artifact_registry_repository\" \"crewai_builder\" {\n  project       = var.gke_project_id\n  location      = var.region\n  repository_id = \"crewai-${var.environment}\"\n  description   = \"Container registry for CrewAI crew builds\"\n  format        = \"DOCKER\"\n\n  labels = local.labels\n}\n\n# ---------------------------------------------------------------------------\n# IAM: Writer Access for BuildKit\n# ---------------------------------------------------------------------------\n# Allows the platform service account to push images during crew builds.\n\nresource \"google_artifact_registry_repository_iam_member\" \"builder_writer\" {\n  project    = var.gke_project_id\n  location   = var.region\n  repository = google_artifact_registry_repository.crewai_builder.name\n  role       = \"roles/artifactregistry.writer\"\n  member     = \"serviceAccount:${google_service_account.crewai_platform.email}\"\n}\n\n# ---------------------------------------------------------------------------\n# IAM: Reader Access for Pod Image Pulls\n# ---------------------------------------------------------------------------\n# Allows the platform service account to pull crew images via Workload Identity.\n# This enables pods to pull images without imagePullSecrets.\n\nresource \"google_artifact_registry_repository_iam_member\" \"platform_reader\" {\n  project    = var.gke_project_id\n  location   = var.region\n  repository = google_artifact_registry_repository.crewai_builder.name\n  role       = \"roles/artifactregistry.reader\"\n  member     = \"serviceAccount:${google_service_account.crewai_platform.email}\"\n}\n"
}
```

```json
{
  "file_path": "/home/nathan_booth/code/konecta-ix-services/kix-services-crewai/environments/dev/terraform/main.tf",
  "content": "# ---------------------------------------------------------------------------\n# CrewAI Platform - Dev Environment\n#\n# Terraform configuration for CrewAI Enterprise deployment on GKE.\n# Composes remote modules from kix-platform-modules plus inline resources.\n#\n# File Organization:\n# - main.tf           : Providers, data sources, locals (this file)\n# - service-accounts.tf: GCP service account and IAM bindings\n# - kubernetes.tf     : Namespaces, K8s service accounts, quotas, limits\n# - secrets.tf        : Secret Manager and K8s secrets/configmaps\n# - storage.tf        : GCS buckets and HMAC keys\n# - database.tf       : Cloud SQL databases, user, and proxy deployment\n# - artifact-registry.tf: Container registry for crew builds\n# - variables.tf      : Input variable definitions\n# - outputs.tf        : Output value definitions\n# - versions.tf       : Provider version constraints\n# - backend.tf        : Remote state configuration\n# ---------------------------------------------------------------------------\n\n# ---------------------------------------------------------------------------\n# Locals\n# ---------------------------------------------------------------------------\n# Common values used across multiple resources.\n\nlocals {\n  # Standard labels applied to all resources for identification and cost tracking\n  labels = {\n    app         = \"crewai\"\n    environment = var.environment\n    managed-by  = \"terraform\"\n    platform    = \"crewai\"\n  }\n\n  # Construct SA email statically to avoid for_each unknown value issues.\n  # This is used by secret-manager modules which need the email before the SA exists.\n  crewai_sa_email = \"crewai-platform-${var.environment}@${var.gke_project_id}.iam.gserviceaccount.com\"\n}\n\n# ---------------------------------------------------------------------------\n# Providers\n# ---------------------------------------------------------------------------\n\nprovider \"google\" {\n  project = var.gke_project_id\n  region  = var.region\n}\n\n# ---------------------------------------------------------------------------\n# Data Sources: GKE Cluster for Kubernetes Provider\n# ---------------------------------------------------------------------------\n# Explicit GKE authentication for reproducible deployments.\n# Avoids reliance on KUBECONFIG environment variable.\n\ndata \"google_client_config\" \"default\" {}\n\ndata \"google_container_cluster\" \"gke\" {\n  project  = var.gke_project_id\n  name     = var.gke_cluster_name\n  location = var.region\n}\n\nprovider \"kubernetes\" {\n  host                   = \"https://${data.google_container_cluster.gke.endpoint}\"\n  token                  = data.google_client_config.default.access_token\n  cluster_ca_certificate = base64decode(data.google_container_cluster.gke.master_auth[0].cluster_ca_certificate)\n}\n"
}
```

```json
{
  "subagent_type": "security-auditor",
  "description": "Security review for PR",
  "prompt": "Perform a security audit of this repository for PR readiness. Check:\n\n1. **Hard-coded secrets**: Search all .tf, .yaml, .yml files for passwords, tokens, API keys, or credentials\n2. **IAM role permissions**: Check if any roles are overly permissive (e.g., roles/owner, roles/editor, broad objectAdmin). Look for least-privilege violations\n3. **Container image signing**: Check if there's container signing/verification in place\n4. **Secret exposure**: Check if any sensitive data could be exposed via outputs or logs\n5. **Network security**: Check for overly permissive network policies or missing security controls\n\nFocus on:\n- environments/dev/terraform/*.tf\n- environments/qa/terraform/*.tf\n- environments/prod/terraform/*.tf\n- environments/*/helm/*.yaml\n- .github/workflows/*.yml\n\nReturn a structured report with:\n- CRITICAL issues (must fix before PR)\n- HIGH issues (should fix)\n- MEDIUM issues (nice to fix)\n- Already-good patterns\n\nDO NOT make changes - just report findings with file paths and line numbers."
}
```

```json
{
  "subagent_type": "terraform-engineer",
  "description": "Terraform standards review",
  "prompt": "Review all Terraform configurations for standards compliance. Check:\n\n1. **Duplicate resources**: Look for resources defined multiple times across environments that should be identical\n2. **Hard-coded values**: Find any hard-coded project IDs, regions, bucket names that should be variables\n3. **Variable consistency**: Compare variables.tf across dev/qa/prod - they should have matching definitions\n4. **Module naming**: Check if module usage is consistent\n5. **Environment parity**: Check if dev has resources that qa/prod are missing (or vice versa)\n\nFocus on:\n- environments/dev/terraform/*.tf\n- environments/qa/terraform/*.tf\n- environments/prod/terraform/*.tf\n\nFor each issue found:\n- Report the file path and line number\n- Explain the fix needed\n- If it's a simple fix (like adding a missing variable), provide the exact code\n\nReturn structured findings as:\n- MUST FIX: Things that will cause errors\n- SHOULD FIX: Things that violate best practices\n- DIFFERENCES: Intentional vs unintentional differences between environments"
}
```
